門間 一夫の 
経済深読み

成長戦略より21 世紀ニッポンの「 ア
ポロ計画」を 
みずほ総合研究所  
エグゼクティブエコノミスト  門間一夫 202 6 年 10 月 27 日

心情的にはノーサレンダー路線 
先月の論考では、①度重なる成長戦略にもかかわらず日本の潜在成長率は低下したこと、②成長戦略
には政策手段の特定が困難という基本問題があるこ と 、③25 年英知を尽くしても結果が出なかった事実
は厳粛に受け止めるべきこと、などを述べた 1。 
それを踏まえ、本稿ではあるべき方向性を考えてみたい。選択肢は、(a)25 年で諦めず50 年でも100
年でもやる、(b)成長目標を達成しやすい水準に下げる、(c)政府のミッションは成長戦略とは別にある
と考える、の 3 つである。選択肢(b)が無難に見えるが、もともと成長戦略は「現状の成長率ではだめ
だ」という認識に基づくものである。現状を明確に上回る成長率を目指さない限り、成長戦略としては
自己矛盾である。 
すると残るのは (a)ノーサレンダー路線、(c)ミッション再考、 のいずれかである。このうちノーサレ
ンダー路線、つまり「成長を諦めない」という不屈の姿勢には人々の共感が得られやすい。しかし、不
屈の志を掲げても、手段の裏付けを欠くという現実は変わらない。手段の裏付けを欠くなら「目標」で
はなく「夢」である。もちろん、夢を語ることが悪いわけではないし、政治家は夢を語るべきという考
えもあるだろう。その夢が偶然にでもかなえば歴史に残る偉業であり、夢なら実現せずとも落胆の度合
いは小さい。ノーサレンダー路線はアップサイドだけでダウンサイドはない、と考えてよいだろうか。 
成長戦略のダウンサイド 
成長戦略にダウンサイドはない、やってみて損はない、と い う前提は実は正しくない。成長戦略は「た
だ」でできるわけではないからだ。例えば、成長戦略には多かれ少なかれネット財政支出が伴う。国債
をどれだけ発行できるのか議論の余地はあるが、いずれにせよ有限である。 
一方、成長戦略は総花的になりやすい。成長を実現する手段がはっきりしない以上、ある政策案を「成
長とは無関係」ときっぱり排除するのも難しいからである。経済界の要望や政治家の信念に基づく様々
な政策案が、成長に少しでも関係しそうなら幅広く正当化される。各種施策の費用対効果について一定

1 門間一夫の経済深読み「成長戦略の本質的な限界」 （2026年 9 月25 日 ）。

### Page 2

2

の分析や議論は行われている。しかし、行われることとそれが有効に機能することは別である。費用対
効果の分析が有効に機能し、各種施策の経済成長へのインパクトが正確に測れていたとしたら、25 年に
わたり成長目標の大幅未達という結果にはならなかったはずである。 
一般に、政策目標の実現に向けてリソースが投入され、目標が実現できなかった場合、そのリソース
投入は失敗だったことになる。チャレンジ自体は無駄ではなかったと言える場合もあるが、過去 25 年
だめだった目標にもう一度挑戦してみるという話なら、たぶん次もだめだろうと事前に予想できる。金
も人もふんだんにあるなら別だが、財政状況が厳しく、人材の不足も叫ばれる国で、高確率で失敗する
ミッションへのリソース投入は賢明な選択と言えるのか。 
有限のリソースが無駄に使われれば、他の課題のために使えるリソースが減る。ここで言うリソース
は財政資金だけではない。実現できない目的のために、多忙な官僚の労力が割かれ、政治家のポリティ
カル ・ キャピタルも消耗すれば、やればできたはずの他の課題への対応まで中途半端に終わってしまう。
成長戦略には他の課題を犠牲にするという「機会費用」が伴うのであり、それが「ただではない」とい
うことのより正確な意味である。「成長を諦めないノーサレンダー路線でいく」という選択は、「50 年で
も 100 年でもその機会費用を払い続けることを覚悟する」という選択でもある。 
何のための経済成長か 
ここで話を一段階前に戻してみよう。そもそもなぜ、経済成長を高めるべきと考えられているのだろ
うか。もちろん、低成長より高成長の方がいいに決まっているので、経済成長を簡単に高められるので
あれば、そんな疑問を持つ必要はない。しかし、経済成長を高めようとすることで機会費用を払い続け
ることになる確率が高いなら、そうまでしてわれわれが最終的に望んでいるものは何なのかという問い
に、きちんと向き合う必要がある。 
経済成長の必要性を訴える主張は、「〇〇の解決には経済成長が不可欠」「低成長のままなら〇〇のよ
うな不都合が起きる」というパターンにおおむね当てはまるように思う。つまり、現在直面している課
題、あるいは将来直面しそうな課題に対応するには、より高い経済成長が必要という認識が成長戦略の
基礎にあると考えられる。念頭に置かれている課題の中身は、社会保障や財政の持続性、行政・医療・
介護サービスの維持、国際的な存在感の回復、生活水準の向上など、論者により様々である。パイを増
やせるなら改善できる課題は多いので、経済成長を高めるべきという主張は万人受けし、政治家には格
好の政策アジェンダになる。 
しかし、「何らかの課題を経済成長で解決する」という考え方は 、「その課題に直接向き合うより経済
成長を高める方が簡単だ」という前提がないと成立しない。その前提が間違っていれば、むしろ経済成
長に頼るがゆえに肝心の課題がいつまでも解決しないことになる。重要な課題であればあるほど、経済
成長と切り離して解決する努力を早く始めるべきではないか。 
例えば、社会保障や財政の持続性が課題なのであれば、給付と負担のバランスや、恵まれている人と
そうでない人の公平感を改善することで、問題の解決を目指す方が良い。人々の生活水準を高めたいと
いう課題を経済成長と切り離すのは少し厄介だが、国民全員が等しく困っているわけではなく、問題の

### Page 3

3

本質は経済格差にある。分配政策により、国民生活の満足感や社会の安定性を全体として改善すること
はできる。 
国際社会における日本の存在感を高めることは、経済成長ではそもそも無理であろう。かつて日本が
世界第2 位のGDP をキープし、経済大国として存在感を示した時代もあった。しかし、あれは冷戦構造
のもとでの日米関係、戦後の高度成長とその余熱、新興国が発展初期段階にあったことなどによる歴史
の偶然であった。GDP の対世界シェアが3～4％程度まで落ち、今後さらに人口が減り、資源・エネルギ
ーに乏しく、特殊な言語を使う島国が、今から経済成長を多少高められたとしても、そのことで示せる
国際パワーは高が知れている。小さくても存在感がある国をどう作るかが、正しいアジェンダ設定だと
思われる。 
21 世紀ニッポンの「アポロ計画」は？ 
日本が課題だらけなのは確かだが、25 年間どうやっても達成できなかった経済成長に、その解決をこ
れ以上頼るのが正解とは思いにくい。逆に、優先課題の解決に国が本気でリソースを投入するなら、そ
こに民間も収益機会を見出し、様々な連鎖反応を通じて経済に好影響が及ぶ可能性も出てくる。 
経済学者マリアナ・マッツカートは著書「ミッション・エコノミー」において、大きな変革は市場メ
カニズムだけでは生み出せず、国家が野心的だが実行可能な課題を設定し、民間の力を引き出す仕組み
を築くことが重要と説く 2。マッツカートは代表例として、1960 年代の米国アポロ計画を挙げる。1969
年 7 月 20 日、 ニール・アームストロングとバズ・オルドリンが月面に降り立ったことで知られる、あの
アポロ計画である。 
日本の成長戦略も「野心的な課題」を掲げたものではあるが、複雑すぎるマクロ経済そのものを課題
の対象にすると、先月の論考で詳しく述べたように、目的と手段の関係性が特定できない。原因と結果
の関係がよくわからない仕事は、責任の所在があいまいで互いに人任せになり、結果にコミットするこ
との効果が得られない。それに対し、「月に人を送る」などのより具体的なミッションの場合、そのため
には何が必要なのかの因果関係を分析・認識しやすい分、結果にコミットすれば人々の力が正しい方向
へ向かう。 
アポロ計画は、半導体、コンピューター、通信、材料工学などの技術を飛躍的に発展させ、その後の
米国の産業基盤を築いたと言われている。また、計画推進の国家機関であった米航空宇宙局 （NASA）は 、
多くの科学技術人材を育成・輩出した。彼らはその後、金融イノベーションなどでも活躍し、NASA 出身
なので「ロケット・サイエンティスト」と呼ばれたことはよく知られている。 
ここで重要なのは、半導体やコンピューターなどの産業育成自体を目的として、政府が補助金で支援
したという話ではないことである。アポロ計画という具体的なミッションがまず存在し、それへの貢献
に適正な対価で報いる仕組みがイノベーターたちを突き動かし、様々な技術や知見が開発、蓄積された
のである。後世に残った産業基盤や人材は、「月に人を送る」というミッションの副産物であった。

2 Mazzucato, Mariana(2021) Mission Economy: A Moonshot Guide to Changing Capitalism, Allen Lane-Penguin 
（関美和・鈴木絵里子訳『ミッション・エコノミー』News Picks、2021 年 ）。

### Page 4

4

一般論として、何らかの課題を解決すれば、経済に必ず大きなプラスの効果が見込める、とまでは言
えない。しかし、上位目標が「課題の解決」なのだから、それが達成された時点でミッションは成功で
あり、投入されたリソースは「有効に使われた」ことになる。そのうえで、マクロ経済にもプラスの波
及効果があればなお良いが、仮にそれがなくても課題が解決された分、以前より望ましい社会になって
いる。 
最大の難関は、優先課題を特定しそれに取り組む社会的モメンタムを生み出すことであり、それには
既存の発想に捉われない強力なビジョンとリーダーシップが要る。アポロ計画にも当初「無謀で財政資
金の無駄使い」という声があった。マッツカートは先ほどの著書の中で、仮に現代風の費用対効果分析
を適用していたら、アポロ計画は採用されず、人類が月面に立つことはなかったと言っている。 
アポロ計画に相当する案件は、どの時代、どの国にも自明なものとしてあるわけではない。 「月に人を
送る」というミッションは、当時の米ソ冷戦状況において、ソ連に宇宙開発で打ち勝つことが米国の死
活問題、という歴史特殊的な認識のもとで生まれた。それでも、よく考えてみれば今の日本にも、人口
の減少 ・ 高齢化や地政学環境の激変など、 人類史上にもまれな歴史特殊的な条件のもとで、 「死活問題」
になりつつある課題はいくつかあるように思う。例えば、エネルギーや食料の安定供給、生活インフラ ・
医療・介護体制の維持・改善などは、次世代の日本人にとってまさに「死活問題」なのではないか。 
限られたリソースの優先課題への配分が、市場メカニズムで実現できないなら、国が強力に関与しな
ければならない。民間には不得意な国民的課題の解決こそ、国の本来の役割であり、そのためのリソー
スを国債発行でまかないきれないなら、国民に追加負担を求めてもいい。 
言うまでもなくこれは困難な作業である。だから優先課題は何か、そのために国民負担を求めるべき
かなどを深く議論せず、経済成長で様々な問題をまとめて解決できることにしておく方が、実はずっと
楽なのである。しかし、それが問題の先送りにすぎないことは繰り返し述べたとおりである。今も成長
戦略が重要だと言い、政府にそれを求めたがるわれわれの意識は、日本が直面する真の課題やその優先
順位を考えるのは面倒だ、 という思考停止から生まれているのかもしれない。21 世紀ニッポンの「アポ
ロ計画」は何なのか、国民的な議論の活性化が必要ではないだろうか。

Document 077: 2027年春闘、初動のモメンタムは現時点で良好 ～労働側、経営側とも昨年からのトーンダウンはみられず～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2027年春闘、初動のモメンタムは現時点で良好 ～労働側、経営側とも昨年からのトーンダウンはみられず～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年10 月 22 日

1/5 
2027 年春闘、初動のモメンタムは現時点で良好 
～労働側、経営側とも昨年からのトーンダウンはみられず～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

（要旨） 
○ 連合は2027 年春闘基本構想で、 定昇込みの賃上げ要求を 「６％中心、５％以上」 、ベア等の
賃上げ分を「４％中心、３％以上」とする目安を示した。前年よりも要求姿勢をやや強めて
おり、５％台の賃上げの定着に加え、さらなる引き上げを目指す姿勢がうかがえる。 
○ 経団連も高い賃上げの継続に前向きな姿勢を維持している。経労委報告の骨子案では賃上げ
モメンタムの「浸透・定着」が掲げられ、高い賃上げの継続に加え、中小企業への波及を重
視する姿勢が示されている。 
○ 日本経済研究センターのＥＳＰフォーキャスト調査によると、民間エコノミストは 2027 年
春闘の賃上げ率を4.96％と予想（厚生労働省ベース）。前年から小幅に鈍化するものの、高
い伸びが続く見通しであり、４年連続で５％台の賃上げを実現できるかが焦点となる。 
○ 春先にはイラン情勢悪化による賃上げ鈍化が懸念されていたが、現時点で労使双方の姿勢に
トーンダウンはみられない。企業業績の底堅さや構造的な人手不足が高い賃上げを支えてお
り、2027 年春闘の初動のモメンタムは良好と評価できる。

連合は、「６％中心、５％以上」と昨年からトーンをやや強める 
2027 年春闘の集中回答日は2027 年の３月中旬だが、実際の交渉に向けての動きは既に始まりつ
つある。 
本日、連合は「2027 春季生活闘争基本構想」を公表した。春闘に向けて最初に注目される重要
イベントであり、翌年の春闘に向けた労働組合側の基本理念や方針、重点課題を示すものであ
る。この基本構想をみることで、労働側が翌年の春闘にどの程度強気な姿勢で臨もうとしている
かを早い段階で確認することができ、春闘全体のトーンをつかむうえで重要な材料となる。 
今回の基本構想の最大の注目点は、定期昇給相当分を含めて「６％中心、５％以上」 、ベースア
ップ等の賃上げ分を「４％中心、３％以上」とし、より高い賃上げを目指す姿勢を示したことで
ある。「５％以上」という要求水準自体は４年連続（2023 年は「５％程度」）となるが、今回は
「６％中心」との表現が新たに加わった。ベア等の賃上げ分についても、従来の「３％以上」に

### Page 2

2/5 
「４％中心」が加えられている。前年の2026 年春闘では、全体で「５％以上」 、中小で「６％以
上」 、有期・短時間・契約等労働者では「７％」を目安とする、いわゆる「５・６・７」の要求が
示されていた。今回、全体についても６％を明確に意識する形となったことは、実質的には５％
を最低ラインとしつつ、より高い賃上げを促す姿勢を強めたものと受け取れる。単に高い賃上げ
を続けるだけでなく、その水準をさらに引き上げようとする意欲がうかがえる。

こうした姿勢の背景にあるのが、ここ数年の高い賃上げ実績である。2026 年春闘では連合ベー
スの賃上げ率の実績が5.01％となり、３年連続で５％台の高い賃上げが実現した。５％という水
準がかつてほど実現困難な目標ではなくなりつつあるなか、連合としても前年と同じ要求目安を
繰り返すだけでは、さらなる賃上げを促すメッセージとして十分ではないと判断した可能性があ
る。５％台の賃上げを４年目も維持するだけでなく、６％近辺を意識して賃上げ率のさらなる引
き上げを目指す段階に入ったというメッセージと位置付けられる。 
賃上げを取り巻く環境も、こうした強気の姿勢を支えている。構造的な人手不足が続くなか、
企業にとって人材の確保・定着は引き続き重要な経営課題であり、賃金引き上げの必要性は高
い。また、物価上昇率は2026 年度後半にかけて再び高まるとみられ、家計の生活コスト負担は重
い。名目賃金が増加しても、物価上昇に追いつかなければ生活水準の改善にはつながらない。実
質賃金を安定的にプラスにするためにも、労働側が高い賃上げを求める理由は十分にある。 
加えて、賃上げの広がりという点でも課題は残る。2026 年春闘では中小組合の賃上げ率が
4.69％と高い伸びを確保したものの、全体の5.01％には届かなかった。大企業と中小企業の間に
はなお差があり、正規雇用と非正規雇用の賃金格差も残っている。連合にとっては、高い賃上げ
春闘年 連合の要求目安（定昇相当分込み） 連合ベース 厚労省ベース
2020 ４％程度 1.90％ 2.00％
2021 ４％程度 1.78％ 1.86％
2022 ４％程度 2.07％ 2.20％
2023 ５％程度 3.58％ 3.60％
2024 ５％以上 5.10％ 5.33％
2025 ５％以上 5.25％ 5.52％
2026 ５％以上 5.01％ 5.18％
2027 ６％中心、５％以上 — —
(出所）連合「春季生活闘争基本構想」、連合「春季生活闘争 第7回（最終）回答集計結果」、厚生労働省「民間主要企業春季賃上げ要求・妥結状況」
連合の賃上げ要求目安と春闘賃上げ率
実績

### Page 3

3/5 
を実現するだけでなく、それを幅広い企業や労働者に波及させることも引き続き重要な課題であ
る。今回の基本構想でも、賃金実態を把握できないなどの事情がある中小組合については
「20,000 円以上・６％以上」 、有期・短時間・契約等労働者については「７％」を目安とする方針
が示された。こうした点を踏まえれば、今回の基本構想は、高い賃上げの持続と一段の引き上
げ、そして賃上げの裾野の拡大を目指すものと評価できる。 
また、今回の基本構想では、新たに2030 年の賃金水準を2026 年比で15％程度引き上げる中期
目標も設定された。毎年の賃上げ率だけでなく、中期的に目指す賃金水準を明示することで、高
い賃上げを一過性のものに終わらせず、継続させようとする姿勢がうかがえる。 
なお、近年は個別組合の実際の賃上げ要求率がすでに６％近くに達しており、 「６％中心」とい
う今回の表現には、こうした実態を追認する意味合いもある。今回の基本構想からは、連合の強
い賃上げ意欲が確認できる一方、実際の賃上げ率が前年を上回るかどうかはまた別問題である。
今後は、主要産業別労働組合組織や個別組合の要求水準が前年からどの程度引き上げられるか
（られないか）が注目される。

経営側も賃上げの継続に前向き 
経営側も高い賃上げの継続に前向きな姿勢を維持している。経団連が毎年１月に公表する「経
営労働政策特別委員会報告（経労委報告） 」は、春闘に向けた経営側の基本スタンスを示すもので
あり、賃上げ交渉の方向性を占う上で重要な資料となる。2027 年春闘に向けた経労委報告につい
ては、現時点で原案は明らかになっていないものの、９月上旬には骨子案の内容が報道されてい
る。それによると、賃上げモメンタムの「浸透・定着」を掲げ、高い賃上げの継続と中小企業へ
の波及を促す方針とされる。 
経団連は2023 年を賃上げの「起点」 、2024 年を「加速」 、2025 年を「定着」 、2026 年を「さらな
る定着」の年と位置付けてきた。今回の「浸透・定着」という表現からは、高い賃上げを継続す
るとともに、その動きを中小企業などに広げることを重視する姿勢がうかがえる。また、経団連
の筒井会長は９月７日の記者会見で、 「ベースアップ実施の検討を引き続きスタンダードと位置付
けていきたい」と述べ、賃上げのモメンタムを維持する考えを示している。 
数か月前には、イラン情勢の悪化による原材料費や物流費の上昇が企業収益を圧迫し、2027 年
春闘では経営側が賃上げに慎重な姿勢を強めることも懸念されていた。しかし、現時点で経営側
の賃上げ姿勢にトーンダウンはみられない。 
なお、経労委報告では、原案から正式な報告にかけての修正は例年あまり見られないが、骨子
案から原案にかけては内容が多少修正されることもある。今後、10 月末～11 月中頃までに報道さ
れるとみられる経労委報告の原案が明らかになれば、経営側のスタンスをより具体的に判断でき
るだろう。

エコノミスト予想は4.96％、５％を巡る攻防か 
10 月14 日に日本経済研究センターが公表した「ＥＳＰフォーキャスト調査」では、民間エコノ

### Page 4

4/5 
ミスト 34 人を対象に2027 年春闘の賃上げ率について特別調査を実施している。これによると、
予測値の平均は4.96％となり、2026 年実績の5.18％を 0.22％ポイント下回る見通しとなった。
もっとも、前年から小幅に鈍化するとはいえ、ほぼ５％という歴史的に高い水準であり、2027 年
も高い賃上げが続くとの見方が中心である。なお、ここでの賃上げ率は厚生労働省「民間主要企
業春季賃上げ要求・妥結状況」ベースであり、連合の集計とは対象が異なる。 
予測の内訳をみると、定期昇給分が1.69％、ベースアップ分が3.27％となっており、ベアにつ
いても３％を超える伸びが見込まれている。また、予測値の高位８機関平均は5.23％、低位８機
関平均は4.68％となっている。予測には一定の幅があるものの、高い賃上げが続くという点では
概ね認識は共有されている。

2027 年春闘で賃上げ率が前年からやや鈍化すると予想される背景には、2026 年の物価上昇率が
2025 年より低く、物価高に対応するための賃上げ圧力が幾分弱まることがあると考えられる。ま
た、数年にわたる高い賃上げによって人件費負担が累積していることに加え、イラン情勢悪化を
背景とした原材料費や物流費の上昇も、企業の賃上げ余力を抑制する要因となり得る。一方、企
業業績が足元まで好調を維持していることや、構造的な人手不足と採用競争の激化は引き続き賃
上げを促す要因であり、賃上げ率が大きく鈍化する可能性も低いとみられる。 
ちなみに、昨年の同時期のＥＳＰフォーキャスト調査でも、2026 年春闘の賃上げ率は4.81％
（2025 年10 月調査）と、前年実績からの鈍化が予想されていた。しかし、実際には5.18％と予
想を上回り、３年連続で５％台の賃上げが実現した。今回も前年からの小幅な鈍化が見込まれて
いるが、予測平均は昨年の同時期をやや上回っている。イラン情勢の悪化という新たな懸念材料
があるなかでも、エコノミストの間では高い賃上げが続くとの見方が維持されていることが分か
（出所）日本経済研究センター「ESPフォーキャスト調査（2026年10月調査）」

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
5/5 
る。 
こうしたなか、予測平均の4.96％について、前年からの「鈍化」を強調するか、5％近傍という
「高水準の持続」を重視するかは、評価が分かれるところだろう。いずれにせよ、2027 年春闘で
は、３年連続で実現した５％台の賃上げを４年目も維持できるか、５％を巡る攻防が一つの焦点
となる。今後、経労委報告原案の判明により経営側の方針が明らかになるほか、主要産業別労働
組合組織の要求方針などが明らかになるにつれて、賃上げ率の見通しもより具体化していくこと
になるだろう。 
なお、この調査とは別に、毎年11 月～12 月にかけて、民間シンクタンク・調査機関から翌年の
春闘賃上げ率の予測が出されることが多い （筆者は 11 月に発表予定） 。 予測をレポートとして作成
することもあれば、 経済見通しレポートのなかで言及されることもあり、春闘賃上げ率の見通しに
ついて、この時期に市場の相場観が徐々に形成されていく。

2027 年春闘の初動は良好 
以上を踏まえると、2027 年春闘の「初動のモメンタム」は、現時点では良好と評価できる。イ
ラン情勢が悪化した数か月前には、原材料費や物流費の上昇による企業収益の圧迫を通じて、
2027 年春闘の賃上げ率が大きく鈍化するとの懸念もあった。しかし、ここまでの動きをみる限
り、労働側、経営側のいずれについても、前年と比べて賃上げ姿勢が慎重化している様子はみら
れない。連合が定昇込み、ベアともに前年より強い要求目安を打ち出し、さらに高い賃上げを志
向する一方、経団連も高い賃上げの継続と中小企業への波及に前向きな姿勢を示している。 
その背景には、原油などの代替調達が進み、資源の調達難が生産活動を大きく制約する事態が
回避されたことや、ＡＩ需要の拡大にも支えられ、企業業績が総じて堅調さを維持していること
があるとみられる。また、構造的な人手不足や採用競争の激化によって、企業が人材確保・定着
のために賃金を引き上げる必要性は依然として高い。物価上昇が続くなか、実質賃金の改善を求
める声が強いことも、高い賃上げに向けての動きを後押ししているとみられる。 
もっとも、原材料費や物流費の上昇による企業収益への悪影響が今後さらに広がる可能性はあ
り、先行きを楽観視することはできない。また、現時点で確認できるのは労使双方の基本的な姿
勢であり、具体的な要求や回答の水準はまだ明らかになっていない。今後は、経団連「経労委報
告」の原案や主要産業別労働組合組織の要求方針、早期に賃上げを表明する企業の動向などを通
じて、今回確認された前向きな姿勢が実際の賃上げにつながるかを見極める必要がある。

2 群益投顧

  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

39% Q/Q, mainly from pricing.
The first phase of capacity expansion is scheduled to enter volume production in
2H27; the additional NT$15.8bn investment could contribute in 2028.

Incremental capacity will favor NAND, in particular 3D NAND-based eMMC (i.e., MLC
NAND), with no planned increase in NOR capacity. The company will gradually
withdraw from ROM product.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Macronix International Co Ltd (2337.TW)
Base case, P/B, in line with our Greater China memory coverage, due to the industry's cyclical
nature. We apply a target multiple of 2.1x to our 2027 BVPS estimate vs. its historical average
of 1.5x since 2017. We continue to believe the P/B target is justifiable, as the MLC and legacy
TLC NAND could be in greater shortage into 2H26, with under-supply easily reaching 40%. We

believe the only supplier that could fill the gap remains Macronix.
Risks to Upside
n Wider legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR up-cycle, driven by limited supply expansion and/or stronger demand
n Faster growth for Switch hardware and core titles
Risks to Downside
n Less severe legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR down-cycle, driven by faster supply expansion and/or demand weakness
n Slower growth for Switch hardware and core titles

STOCK RATINGS

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 results preview – all eyes
on AI ASIC  development
MediaTek remains competitive in the AI ASIC service industry
given strong execution and design capability. We look forward to
3Q26 updates.
We expect US$2bn revenue of AI ASIC in 4Q26: MediaTek will host its 3Q26
earnings call on 10/30 Friday. It posted 10% Q/Q growth in preliminary 3Q26
revenue. Into 4Q26, given weak smartphone and consumer (eg. TV) demand, we

expect non-AI revenue to decline 10%-20% Q/Q. That said, thanks to US$2bn of TPU
v8t (ZebraFish) mass production, we expect 4Q26 revenue to grow 30%-40% Q/Q.
For gross margin, we expect 4Q26 to decline to 44-45%, as AI ASIC will likely dilute
the corporate margin.  Please see Exhibit 1   for our earnings preview.
Key focus for the call – all about AI ASIC (see next section for 2028 AI ASIC):
1. 2027 AI ASIC TAM and MediaTek’s market share: We expect MediaTek to

modestly raise its 2027 AI ASIC TAM or market share outlook, given better visibility
on TPU v8t (ZebraFish) CoWoS and substrate supply. Our  Asia supply chain checks
suggest 180k of CoWoS capacity could be allocated to TPU v8t, supporting
production of  3.6mn units, of which we assume 3mn units will be recognized as
MediaTek revenue in 2027. If substrate availability improves, we believe TSMC’s
CoWoS allocation to the TPU v8t could increase to 200k-220k units.

2. Google TPU v9 status (covered by Brian Nowak): This project is MediaTek's major
2028 revenue driver, and where investors appeared concerned, given uncertainty
around Intel (covered by Joe Moore) EMIB-T substrate supply. We expect Mediatek
to share that the TPU v9 (HumuFish) project is roughly on schedule for early 2027
tape-out, with high-volume production starting in early 2028, as previously guided.
We previously discussed the possibility of MediaTek using TSMC's CoWoS to help

with any 2028 TPU production concerns, and TSMC management indicated they
would be willing to help customers to smooth production if they were struggling
with EMIB-T supply. If MediaTek did want to tape out another CoWoS packaging
version of TPU v9, we believe it could take around a year based on existing norms.
Whether MediaTek adopts CoWoS for TPU v9, or waits for the next generation
(IceFish, or TPU v10) as a back-up, it currently is only using EMIB-T.

3. Google TPU v10 competition: As we described in Google ASIC, who gets what
for design services?, we see GUC and Alchip as partners for MediaTek coordinating
around the TPU v10 IceFish design service.
4. Second customer win? In our Nvidia partnership note, we saw a higher chance of
MediaTek winning a second or even third CSP customer (MediaTek had indicated it
was engaging with some customers, Aug. 31 interview). MediaTek may also consider
China CSP projects if its 3nm wafer allocation is sufficient for 2027-28, and projects

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.

its new pricing from September. We expect TPU to be the main growth driver for 4Q26
revenue while non-TPU revenue could see a   QoQ decline into 4Q26, given the demand
pull forward and consumer demand weakness.

1. Substrate supply is the key, not demand, for MediaTek
production for Google TPUs
TSMC CoWoS could   support 2nm (Humufish) MediaTek's production for TPUs in 2028:

In our earlier report, MediaTek: 2Q26 results preview: Smartphone weak, but Cloud AI
taking off; OW (26 Jul 2026), we noted that, in our view,  MediaTek might secure a portion
of CoWoS capacity for 2nm TPUs to ensure minimum volume production. However, we
believe, in-line with MediaTek's intention, it will use EMIB-T from Intel  as the primary
solution to reduce costs and scale volume in 2028, if execution proceeds well.
During TSMC's earlier earnings call, TSMC management shared that it was willing to help

customers that may be struggling with Intel EMIB-T supply to smooth production, which
suggested to us that it would be willing to offer support even if CoWoS were the back-up/
second source. However, our recent industry checks suggest that MediaTek may not need
to adopt TSMC CoWoS for its TPU v9 as the EMIB-T substrate (for v9) output is tracking
its plan. If MediaTek did decide to use CoWoS, we believe the tape-out of the CoWoS
version of TPU v9 could take a year, based on previous norms.

CoWoS constraints eased, but ABF substrate supply still the gating factor for TPU
production bull case: In our recent AI supply chain tracker, we noted that MediaTek’s
180k CoWoS-S bookings imply ~3.6 units of TPU 8t (ZebraFish) in 2027, above our original

3mn shipment assumption. However, we continue to bake in some conservatism as we had
assumed ABF substrate shortages. So,  we still forecast 3mn units in 2027.

2. Could MediaTek add second and third AI ASIC customers?
Through the NVLink Fusion platform, MediaTek's XPU customers can leverage NVIDIA's
(covered by Joe Moore) scale-up and scale-out networking technologies to build AI server

systems. Our Asia supply chain checks suggest MediaTek may target subsystem builds for
a second CSP customer rather than supplying the XPU itself. To account for the impact of
the NVLink Fusion platform, we now assume an initial revenue contribution in 2028,
based on approximately 100k subsystem units at an ASP of US$25k, which is a rough
estimate based on materials needed, including HBM.
We therefore assume a US$2.5bn revenue contribution in 2028, based on 100k units and

an ASP of US$25k, including HBM chip consignment, known-good dies (KGDs) for
compute chips, and chiplets.
On margin trends, management highlighted continued value creation for customers across
both silicon and packaging design. As a result, we expect ASICs to remain accretive to
operating margins. At the gross margin level, management indicated that margins vary by
project, potentially reflecting support for full-rack systems. We model 2nm TPU gross

margins for MediaTek at c.35% (vs. c.40% for 3nm TPUs) given the larger revenue scale.
We continue to expect operating margins of ~20–25%, above the smartphone business
unit's 15–20%.

3. Why could TSMC be motivated  to support MediaTek and
Google?
TSMC's CoWoS-L solution carries a structurally higher cost vs. EMIB due to its larger
reticle size, as we discussed in The Foundry Floorplan: TSMC preview update; CoWoS vs.
EMIB competition (14 Apr 2026). We think TSMC may want to address the growing
competition from Intel's EMIB-T, for which we estimate 40-45k wafer equivalent capacity

in 2028 based on our Asia supply chain checks, implying a 10-15% share of the 2.5D
packaging market.
For MediaTek to adopt TSMC's CoWoS-L as a second source alongside EMIB-T, the RDL
(redistribution layer) connection to the interposer requires tape-out of three additional
photomask layers. However, our Asia channel checks suggest that Google may be willing
to absorb the incremental costs associated with dual sourcing, which could allow
MediaTek to sustain its gross margin.

We think MediaTek may produce 3mn units for TPUv9 shipments in
2028 with support from Intel's EMIB-T
Based on Asia supply chain checks, average EMIB-T capacity is around 5kwpm in 2026,
which we expect to further scale to 15-20kwpm in 2027 and 40-45kwpm in 2028. All of
the capacity mentioned above is converted for 12-inch wafer equivalent for customers'

easier understanding compared  with TSMC's CoWoS capacity.
Although some small projects are ongoing, we still see MediaTek/Google Humufish
consuming most of the capacities during 2027-28. We now expect MediaTek to produce
3mn units of TPUv9 (Humufish) with support from Intel's EMIB-T from late 2027-2028.
We see the current average EMIB-M capacity at 110kwpm, based on supply chain checks,
which could slightly decrease to 95kwpm in 2027 as Intel is converting some capacities

into EMIB-T.

Relative valuation
We view the P/E as relatively attractive – even after the share price rally – given strong AI
demand. Although MediaTek faces smartphone headwinds in 2026, we believe it is time to
look beyond 2026. We think this margin pressure is fully priced in, while TPU upside is
not. We believe the stock should trade more than +1SD above its historical average,
supported by strong TPU demand. Our  price target of NT$6,188 implies 42x  2027 EPS, on

our estimates.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside

n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.
n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Idea
3Q26 earnings missed: Please see Exhibit 1 .
4Q guidance reflects weak NB demand: Parade is guiding rev. to decline 24% Q/Q
at mid-point, 32% below consensus. We overestimated the stability of demand from
standard-plus customers, while demand from standard customers remains very
weak, as expected. Based on the current demand outlook into 1Q27, it is difficult to
see a V-shaped recovery. That said, we appreciate Parade's supply chain
management and its ability to pass cost pressures on to customers. We see Parade's

TTED business offering L T opportunities amid PCB price hikes.
New business ramp could take time: We previously modelled the company’s ASIC-
like business contributing 15-20% of revenue in 2027-28. Given the complexity of
the chip design and weak DT demand, we believe we were too optimistic. As for the
TTED business, the company expects shipments to exceed 5mn units in 2026, which
could account for ~8% of 2026 revenue. The company expects NB shipments to

remain similar in 2027 vs. 2026, subject to DRAM pricing. Currently, we do not
expect DRAM pricing to decline meaningfully.
Double downgrade to UW; PT NT$444: We cut our earnings forecasts mainly to
reflect weaker 4Q guidance and a lower contribution from the ASIC-like business.
We lowered 2026/27/28e earnings by 14%/39%/43%. The company is now trading at
17x 2027e EPS. We like Parade's long-term growth potential but would need to see a
meaningful resumption in business growth to turn more positive. (continued on the

next page)

 Idea
2

3Q26 earnings review: 3Q revenue was NT$4.2bn, -2% Q/Q, -4% Y/Y, and 4% above MSe; 
3Q GM was 40.3%, flattish Q/Q, -2.2ppt Y/Y and 2.3ppt below MSe; 3Q EPS was NT$7.67, 
flat Q/Q, -27% Y/Y and 6% below MSe. Weaker 2H26 demand has been due to OEM pull-
ins in 1H, compressed consumer demand due to price hikes, and compressed enterprise 
budgets due to allocation to AI spending.
Exhibit 1: 3Q26 earnings review
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research (E) estimates
Why it's not too late to downgrade now: The weak 4Q26 outlook lowers the earnings 
base, while visibility on a sustained recovery remains limited. With NB shipments expected 
to remain broadly flat in 2027, TTED growth and initial ASIC-like contributions may be 
insufficient to offset weakness in the core business. The risk of prolonged earnings 
pressure and a slower-than-expected ramp in new businesses supports our more cautious 
view beyond the near-term reset.

results, the lower 4Q guidance than we had expected, and the delays ramping up the new
business.

Parade: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target from  NT$818 to NT$444:  Our price target is our base case
scenario value, which we continue to derive from a residual income model. Our bull and
bear case scenario values are cut from NT$948 to NT$755, and from NT$418 to NT$288,
respectively.
Intermediate growth cut to 9.4% from 11.4% to reflect slower ramp up of new businesses.
Other key RI assumptions are unchanged:
• 9.8% cost of equity (beta 1.3, risk-free rate 2% and risk premium 6%)

• Payout ratio of 50%
• Terminal growth rate of 4.0%

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Document 130: 261029_daiwa_Delta

Source PDF: all report pdf/261029_daiwa_Delta.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide our latest views on Delta after its 3Q26 results 
call on 29 October.

What’s the impact: Business outlook. During the results call, Delta 
remained positive on AI monetisation after its analysis of Anthropic’s S-1 
filing. Management flagged shortages of components, power and skilled 
electricians and slower-than-expected data centre buildout to be potential 
hiccups in the process; however, it still sees strong underlying demand. As 
such, we still expect sequential revenue growth in both 4Q26E and 1Q27E 
as component shortages had deferred 3Q26 demand. On rising material 
costs, Delta noted that BoM costs have increased by 2% as of 3Q26 (vs. 
early 2026), with the company gradually passing these costs through to 
end customers in 4Q26 and 1Q27. We believe the main gross margin 
driver will likely still be the absence of inventory write-offs and a higher 
power mix in 4Q26E. We forecast a gross margin recovery to 36% and a 
record operating margin of 18.5% in 4Q26. As for long-term growth, we 
maintain our investment theses that DC-DC module and HVDC adoption 
will become key growth drivers for Delta in 2027 and 2028, respectively.

3Q26 results missed on gross margin. Delta's 3Q26 earnings were 
TWD23.5bn (EPS: TWD9.04), missing our estimate of TWD27.3bn and 
Bloomberg consensus estimate of TWD29.6bn by 14% and 21%, 
respectively. We attribute the weakness mainly to lower-than-expected 
gross margin, driven by: 1) higher-than-expected inventory write-offs amid 
product shipment hiccups; 2) a one-off provision for customer claims, with 
the related loss likely to ultimately be borne by the supplier; and 3) rising 
material costs. Based on our calculation, Delta recognised c.TWD4bn of 
inventory provision loss in 3Q26, which reduced gross margin by 2pp. 
Excluding inventory provision loss, gross margin was c.35.8%, close to our 
previous estimate of 36.3%. 3Q26 revenue reached TWD197.1bn (up 8% 
QoQ and 31% YoY), which was also negatively affected by product 
shipment hiccups (vs. our previous estimate of 10% QoQ growth).

What we recommend: We trim our 2026-28E EPS by 2-6% to reflect lower 
revenue assumptions due to the impact of component shortages. We 
reaffirm our Buy (1) call but trim our 12M TP to TWD3,000 (from 
TWD3,088) based on an unchanged target PER of 49x (high-end of its 3-
year range of 11-50x) applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key 
downside risk: lower-than-expected demand.

How we differ: Our 2028E revenue is 12% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely on our more bullish assumption regarding HVDC 
adoption.

29 October 2026

3Q26 review: margin likely bottomed-out

➢ Gross margin to recover on lower inventory provisions in 4Q26 
➢ Addressable market remains intact despite some near-term hiccups   
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; trimming 12M TP to TWD3,000

Company profile 
Founded in 1971, Delta Electronics has been the global leader in switching power supply solutions since 
2002 and DC brushless fans since 2006. Its business covers 3 key areas: power electronics (eg, power 
supplies, thermal management products and passive components), automation (eg, controllers, robot 
arms and building management systems), and infrastructure (eg, datacentre infrastructure, networking 
and EV charging).

### Page 5

5

Delta Electronics (2308 TT): 29 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 30 Sep 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Delta's board has 7 non-independent and 5 independent directors with a term of three years.
The total number of directors and ratio of independent directors appear higher than most other
listed companies. Besides, according to Delta's CSR report, enhancing the board's functions is
one of the key goals for the company to achieve better corporate governance; also, the board
members' diversity and professionalism will be taken into consideration to ensure a good board
quality. Moreover, the company evaluates all the directors in the board using internal regulations
and an external independent institution each year and at least once every three years,
respectively. We believe Delta has implemented relatively more enhanced measure than most
listed firms to improve its board quality.
Capital management 2
During 2016-19, Delta's payout ratio was stable in the range of c.70%, but fell to 56% in 2020
and 53% in 2021. For 2020, it was mainly due to a sizeable non-cash re-valuation gain after the
acquisition of Delta Thailand (DET), while the company maintained more cash on hand to deal
with the uncertainties caused by COVID-19. However, the DPS growth from 2016 to 2021 was
limited, as Delta struggled with business diversification into new end-markets (eg, smartphone,
NEV). We believe that Delta's effort started to bear fruit in recent years after its NEV business
has gradually picked up. Overall, we see a stable dividend policy from Delta and expect its DPS
to continue to grow YoY in the future given that they declared a TWD11.6 DPS for 2025 which
equals a 50% payout ratio.
Related party &
transaction 1 Revenue from related parties stood at 12% of total 2025 revenue and purchases from relatedparties were at 11%. We see limited risk from related party transactions.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
Delta focuses on: 1) localised management, 2) supplier certification and risk management, and
3) supplier sustainability engagement as key initiatives under the scope of supply chain
management. In 2019, 144 new suppliers joined Delta's supply chain system. All of them passed
environmental and social evaluations, signed the declaration of "non-use of conflict materials",
and complied with Responsible Business Alliance's (BRA) Code of Conduct. Also, these vendors
had to implement and abide by Delta's supply chain CSR policy and code of conduct. To lower
ESG risks, Delta carries out RBA audits and monitors high-risk critical suppliers each year. The
suppliers that fail these audits have to provide detailed improvement plans in response to
identified issues. Besides, Delta has surveyed a total of 263 suppliers with potential risk and 63
suppliers with high risk of the total 1,331 suppliers, based on its CSR policy and code of conduct.
If these suppliers are flagged as high-risk and no concrete improvements are made, Delta may
consider terminating cooperation and transactions with them to improve the company's
engagement on supplier sustainability. Overall, we think Delta has a solid plan to improve its
supply chain management with specific KPIs, including: 1) achieving supplier improvement rate
of 80% in 2025, 2) reducing GHG emissions rate of critical suppliers by 10% in 2025, and 3)
improving water saving rate of critical suppliers by 12% in 2025.
S Data security 2
Delta's Information Security team created Delta's Information Security Policy in November 2013
and started providing educational training to global employees in 2014. Delta gradually
introduced the Information Security Management System (ISMS) in 2016 and obtained the ISO
27001 international certification in 2018. According to ISO27001, Delta has to conduct at least
one internal audit on information security and appoint an independent institution to conduct audit
externally. According to Delta, no major deficiencies have been found in recent years.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Delta Electronics (2308 TT): 29 October 2026

### Page 1

Summary: On 29 October, Realtek reported 3Q26 results that missed our estimates but 
were in line with the consensus. This was mainly due to weaker-than-expected revenue 
likely on a deeper demand rebalancing despite pricing power on supply-chain cost 
passthrough. While 4Q26 outlook looks intact, management expects steady topline growth 
into 2027, supported by a multi-year bandwidth upgrade cycle, continued pricing resilience 
and emerging AI opportunities from cloud to edge to physical AI, despite likely still subdued 
PC and consumer electronics demand. We rate the stock Outperform (2) premised upon 
our key thesis of a multi-year/multi-G (gigabits per second) bandwidth upgrade cycle 
across wireline and wireless to benefit Realtek, which is further echoed by management’s 
2027 outlook.

Highlight 
• 3Q26 results miss on lower topline. Realtek reported 3Q26 net profit of TWD4.1bn, 
missing our estimate by 7% while in line with the consensus. With financial metrics such 
as margins broadly in line, the miss was mainly due to lower-than-expected topline 
which came in 8% below our estimate at TWD37bn. We believe the weak revenue was 
driven by a deeper demand rebalancing after customer restocking and pre-stocking in 
1H26 on seasonality and supply-chain uncertainties, flagged in our note, Downgrading: 
pricing to bridge rebalancing but …, 4 August. Although Realtek raised prices to pass on 
cost inflation to the supply chain, it failed to offset such rebalancing. As usual, 
management gave no quantitative guidance for 4Q26 but expressing “prudent and 
steady” revenue run rates, in line with our expectations. We will be reviewing our model 
in due course.

• Key takeaways. At today’s results call, management further expected a steady topline 
growth heading into 2027, driven by: 1) multiple-G bandwidth upgrade cycles across 
wireline and wireless networking, including Wi-Fi 7 and 8, multi-G Ethernet, PON and 
switch, to enhance product mix with market-share gains adding to upside; 2) likely 
sustained pricing power with the company continuing to pass on inflated costs to the 
supply chain; and 3) despite likely still subdued demand from PC and consumer 
electronics markets, the ongoing development of AI, from cloud to edge AI to physical AI 
applications, is creating growth opportunities and tallying with our investment thesis of 
the multi-year/multi-G bandwidth upgrade cycle to structurally benefit Realtek.

Recommendation 
We rate Realtek Outperform (2). The stock price has generally consolidated around 
TWD670-790 over the past 3 months due to lack of catalysts amid its demand rebalancing, 
suggesting patience for investors. Key downside risk to our call would be any commodity 
price hikes leading to worse-than-expected demand rebalancing.

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.15  
잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기

3Q26 Preview 
기아는 3Q26E 매출액 31.1조원(+8.2% YoY, -6.0% QoQ), 영업이익 2.2조원
(+53.1% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 7.2%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 
물량은 좋았으나, 아쉬운 믹스와 환율 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.8% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.0만대
(+8.6%)를 기록했다. EV·HEV 확대에 따른 ASP 상승은 지속되고 있다. 전쟁으로 인
한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 재료비 부담은 전분기 대비 3,000억~4,000억원 증가할 
것으로 예상된다. EV 판매 비중은 2분기 13%에서 3분기 15%, 유럽은 35%에서 
43%로 확대되며 유럽 내 마진 희석은 불가피하다. 원화 강세에 따른 영업상 부담은 
판매보증충당부채 환입 등으로 상쇄돼 3분기 환율 영향은 제한적일 전망이다. 판매보
증충당부채 환입은 QoQ +5,000억원 수준으로 추정된다. 4분기부터 환율 효과는 부정
적일 것이며, 환율 10원 변동 시 연간 손익 민감도는 약 1,500억원 수준이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 165,000원으로 하향 
미국 조지아 공장의 텔루라이드 HEV(2.8만대)와 HMGMA의 스포티지 HEV(1.0만대) 
신규 생산으로 북미의 견조한 HEV 수요에 대응하며, 2027년 믹스 개선이 기대된다. 
유럽은 EV3·EV5로 점유율을 확대하는 가운데 유럽 내 할인율 하락과 EV 잔존가치 
회복으로 판매 여건이 개선되고 있다. 향후 IAA 규제에 따른 법인·플릿 혜택 제한은 
중국 업체의 원가 우위를 줄일 수 있을 전망이다. 
원화 약세 지속을 반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다.  
다만, 현재 주가는 12M Fwd P/E 기준 4.8배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 
수준, 저가 매수 기회라 판단된다. 현재 배당 수익률은 6% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높은 
배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr

### Page 1

2026년 10월 29일 
기업분석

SK하이닉스 (000660) 
높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클

Buy(유지) 
목표주가 3,150,000원, 현재 주가(10/28): 1,487,000원 
▶ Analyst 박준영 jyp94@hanwha.com 3772-7481 / RA 김나우 now.kim@hanwha.com 3772-7710

3Q26 Review: HBM4 출하 확대와 예상보다 강한 가격 상승 
분기 매출액 104.6조원(QoQ +32%, YoY +328%), 영업이익 83.3조원(QoQ +38%, YoY +632%)을 기록하며 시장 기대
치를 상회함. 영업이익률은 전분기 대비 약 3%p 상승한 80%로 사상 최대 분기 실적을 달성함. 특히 DRAM ASP가 전
분기 대비 20% 후반 상승하며 당사 기존 예상치인 +23%를 상회한 점에 주목할 필요가 있음. NAND ASP 역시 20% 
중반 상승하며 예상보다 강한 가격 흐름을 나타냄. DRAM ASP 상승에는 기존 DRAM의 가격 인상과 함께 3분기부터 
본격화된 HBM4 양산 및 출하 확대에 따른 제품 믹스 개선 효과가 반영됨. 서버 DDR5 판매량 역시 전분기 대비 40% 
증가하며 실적 성장을 뒷받침함. 특히 HBM4는 기존 HBM3E 대비 높은 기술적 난도를 반영한 가격으로 공급되고 있어 
향후 출하 비중 확대에 따른 추가적인 ASP 상승 효과도 기대됨. 
이번 실적에서 중요한 부분은 예상보다 높은 가격 상승폭이 2027년 실적 추정치의 상향 가능성을 높이고 있다는 점임. 3
분기 DRAM ASP가 기존 추정치를 상회한 가운데 4분기에도 HBM4 출하 확대와 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 이어질 
것으로 예상되기 때문임. 동사는 4분기 DRAM 출하량이 전분기 대비 20% 중반 증가할 것으로 전망했으며, HBM4 출
하량이 HBM3E를 넘어 주력 제품으로 자리 잡을 것으로 예상함. 특히 HBM4의 양산 수율이 당초 목표를 상회하고 있
으며 높은 고객 수요에 대응하기 위해 생산성 극대화에 집중하고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. HBM4 출하 확대에 따른 믹스 
개선과 기존 DRAM 가격 상승이 동시에 진행되는 가운데 HBM과 일반 DRAM의 수익성 격차 역시 유의미하게 축소
될 것으로 예상됨. 이는 특정 제품에 대한 수익성 의존도가 낮아지면서 DRAM 사업 전반의 이익 체력이 강화되고 있음
을 의미함. 메모리 산업의 높은 영업 레버리지를 고려하면 예상보다 높은 ASP와 출하량 증가는 2027년 이익 추정치의 
추가 상향으로 연결될 가능성이 높다고 판단함.

공급 부족의 장기화와 LTA 확대, 메모리 산업의 이익 구조 변화 
이번 컨퍼런스콜에서 확인된 또 다른 중요한 변화는 공급 부족의 장기화와 장기공급계약(L TA) 확대임. 동사는 첨단 공정
의 기술적 난도 상승과 신규 생산시설 구축에 소요되는 시간을 고려할 때 메모리 공급이 수요를 충족하지 못하는 환경이 
2030년까지 지속될 것으로 전망함. 특히 글로벌 주요 고객사 14곳과 장기공급계약을 체결했으며, 2027~2028년 전체 생
산능력의 50% 이상에 해당하는 물량을 확보함. HBM의 경우 2029년까지의 물량이 이미 판매 확정된 상황으로 중장기 
수요 가시성이 상당히 높아졌다고 판단함. 주목할 부분은 L TA가 단순한 공급물량 확보를 넘어 메모리 업체의 사업 구조
와 이익 안정성을 변화시키고 있다는 점임. 계약 가격은 고객 및 제품별로 차이가 있으나 합의된 범위 내에서 시장 상황
을 반영하는 구조로 운영되고 있으며 예치금 등 실질적인 계약 이행력을 높이는 장치도 포함됨. 이는 장기적인 판매물량
을 확보하면서도 업황 상승에 따른 가격 인상 효과를 일정 부분 누릴 수 있다는 점에서 긍정적임.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

2 
특히 전체 생산능력의 50% 이상을 L TA로 확보하면서 나머지 물량은 시장 상황에 따라 탄력적으로 대응할 수 있다는 점
에 주목할 필요가 있음. 장기계약을 통해 안정적인 매출 기반을 확보하는 동시에 미계약 물량을 활용해 추가적인 가격 
상승에 대응할 수 있기 때문임. 물론 장기계약이 메모리 가격 하락을 완전히 방어하는 것은 아니지만, 과거와 비교해 물
량 가시성과 고객사와의 협상력이 동시에 개선되고 있다는 점은 분명함. 과거 메모리 산업이 높은 이익을 창출하는 국면
에서도 낮은 밸류에이션을 적용 받았던 이유 중 하나는 이익의 지속성에 대한 불확실성이었음. 그러나 장기계약 확대와 
공급 제약의 장기화가 동시에 진행된다면 시장이 평가하는 이익의 지속성 역시 높아질 수 있음. 따라서 향후 주가 상승
은 단순한 EPS 추정치 상향뿐 아니라 이익 변동성 축소에 따른 밸류에이션 재평가가 동시에 진행되는 형태로 나타날 가
능성이 높다고 판단함. 
AI 인프라 투자에 대한 우려 역시 중장기 메모리 수요를 훼손할 가능성은 제한적이라고 판단함. 동사는 주요 하이퍼스케
일러의 클라우드 매출이 전년 대비 30~80% 성장하고 있으며 기업용 AI 솔루션 계약 확대와 클라우드 수주잔고 증가를 
통해 AI 투자의 사업적 성과가 확인되고 있다고 설명함. 특히 초기 AI 투자가 소수 빅테크에 집중되었던 것과 달리 최근
에는 일반기업의 자체 인프라 구축, 소버린 AI, 온프레미스 등으로 투자 주체가 다변화되고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. 특정 
사업자의 투자 속도 조절이 전체 AI 인프라 수요에 미치는 영향은 과거 대비 완화될 수 있기 때문임. AI 투자에 대한 우
려가 지속되는 상황에서도 실제 메모리 수요와 고객사들의 장기계약 확대가 확인되고 있다는 점에서 메모리 업황의 구조
적 성장에 대한 긍정적인 시각을 유지함.

HBM4 경쟁력 강화와 CAPEX 확대, 이익 성장과 주주환원의 선순환 
HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대와 신규 생산능력 확보 역시 2027년 실적 성장의 중요한 기반이 될 전망임. 동사는 M15X
의 생산능력을 연말까지 최대 수준으로 확대할 계획이며 HBM4의 양산 수율 역시 당초 목표를 상회하는 등 안정적인 
생산체계를 확보하고 있음. 특히 HBM 수요가 공급능력의 두 배를 상회하는 상황에서 고객사들의 높은 선호가 이어지고 
있다는 점은 경쟁력 유지에 긍정적임. 최근 일부 고객사들이 HBM 적층 단수를 낮추는 방안을 검토하고 있으나 이는 공
급 부족 상황에서 제한된 물량을 효율적으로 활용하기 위한 전략으로 판단됨. 적층 단수 조정이 전체 HBM 수요 감소로 
직결되는 것은 아니며 오히려 후공정 생산능력에 대한 요구가 확대될 가능성도 존재함. HBM4E와 커스텀 HBM 개발 
역시 고객사 일정에 맞춰 진행되고 있어 차세대 제품에서도 경쟁력을 유지할 것으로 기대됨. 
한편 2027년 CAPEX는 선단 공정 전환과 용인 클러스터를 비롯한 국내외 생산 인프라 확충으로 전년 대비 증가할 전망
임. 다만 CAPEX 확대를 과거와 같은 공급 과잉의 신호로 해석할 필요는 없다고 판단함. 선단 공정의 기술적 난도 상승
과 고부가 제품 비중 확대에 따라 투자금액 증가가 실제 생산량 증가로 정비례하지 않기 때문임. 특히 동사는 신규 팹 
건설 일정을 앞당기면서도 클린룸은 순차적으로 개방하고 실제 생산능력 확대는 최종 수요와 수익성을 고려해 단계적으
로 진행할 계획임. 고객사의 투자 계획뿐 아니라 재무건전성과 자금조달 여력, 공급망 준비 상황까지 점검해 CAPEX 집
행에 반영한다는 점도 긍정적임. 이는 AI 인프라 투자 확대에 대응하면서도 과거 메모리 산업의 공급 과잉 위험을 최소
화하기 위한 전략으로 판단함. 
동사는 이에 더해 2026년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원 규모의 주주환원 계획을 발표했음. 현금배당 30조원 이상, 자사주 
매입 및 소각 60조원 이상으로 구성되며, 기존에 약속했던 잉여현금흐름(FCF)의 50% 이상을 주주에게 환원한다는 원칙
을 구체화한 것임. 지난 8월 발표한 40조원 규모의 자사주 매입은 10월 21일 완료됐으며, 해당 주식은 향후 소각될 예정
임. 이번 3분기 이사회에서는 현금배당 14조원과 추가 자사주 매입 20조원을 결의했으며, 자사주 매입액 중 10조원은 전
량 소각하고 나머지 10조원은 임직원 보상에 활용할 계획임. 임직원 보상용 자사주는 주주환원 규모에서 제외됨. 특히 
향후에도 자사주 매입을 지속할 계획이라는 점에서 주주환원 정책의 지속성이 강화됐다는 판단임.

### Page 3

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

3 
3분기 주당 배당금은 2만원으로, 실적 개선을 반영한 일반배당 1만원과 키옥시아 투자수익을 활용한 특별배당 1만원으
로 구성됨. 총 배당 규모는 약 14조원이며 회사는 올해 실현한 키옥시아 투자수익의 대부분을 3분기 및 연말 특별배당을 
통해 주주에게 환원할 계획임. 이번 결정은 메모리 가격 상승과 고부가 제품 확대에 따른 현금창출력 개선이 실질적인 
주주환원 확대로 연결되고 있음을 보여줌. 특히 2027년 설비투자 확대가 예상되는 상황에서도 대규모 현금배당과 자사
주 소각을 병행한다는 점은 재무적 여력에 대한 자신감을 반영하는 것으로 판단됨. 향후에도 안정적인 실적 성장과 FCF 
확대가 이어질 경우 주주환원의 지속 가능성이 높아질 전망이며 이는 과거 대비 높아진 이익 창출력과 함께 중장기 밸류
에이션 재평가를 뒷받침하는 요인으로 작용할 것으로 예상됨. 
결국 이번 실적발표에서 확인된 핵심은 예상보다 강한 메모리 가격 상승과 HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대, 그리고 장기계
약을 통한 이익 가시성 개선임. 특히 HBM4의 높은 가격과 안정적인 양산 수율, 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 동시에 확
인되면서 2027년 이익 추정치의 추가 상향 가능성이 높아졌다고 판단함. 여기에 L TA 확대에 따른 중장기 이익 안정성 
개선과 대규모 주주환원까지 더해지고 있음. 실적 추정치 상향과 이익의 지속성 개선이 동시에 진행되고 있다는 점에서 
긍정적인 투자 시각을 유지함.

### Page 4

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 29 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성됐음을 확인합니다. 
본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 자료를 
기관투자자 또는 제3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (박준영, 김나우) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 5

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

5 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

### Page 1

SK 하이닉스 (000660/KS) 
여전히 강력하다 
3Q26 영업이익 83 조원으로 예상 상회 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 83조원 (+38% QoQ)으로 예상을 상회했다. 메모
리 공급 부족 심화, HBM4 반영 시작에 따른 메모리 가격 상승이 환율 하락을 상쇄했
기 때문이다. 출하 DRAM +10%, NAND -1%, 가격 DRAM +28%, NAND 
+24%, 영업이익률 DRAM 82%, NAND 72%, HBM 판가는 HBM4 출하가 시작되
며 37% 수준 상승한 것으로 추정된다. 업황은 여전히 예상을 상회하고 있다.

4Q26 분기 영업이익 100 조원 시대 진입 전망 
4Q26 영업이익은 105조원 (+27% QoQ)으로 분기 100조원 시대에 진입할 것으로 
전망한다. 메모리 가격 강세가 지속되는 가운데, 1c 출하 가속화, M15X 램프업 본격화 
등으로 출하량 상승이 기존 예상을 상회할 것이다. 또한 HBM4의 Cross-over 시작 
및 수율 안정화로 HBM 가격, 수익성 제고 동반이 지속될 것이다. 출하는 DRAM 
+24%, NAND +4%, 가격은 DRAM +8%, NAND +7%를 예상한다.

2027 년 영업이익 488 조원 (+70% YoY)으로 상향조정 
SK하이닉스의 2027년 영업이익을 488조원 (+70% YoY)으로 12% 상향 조정한
다. 원/달러 환율 전망치를 기존 1,340원에서 1,315원으로 하향 조정했음에도, 공급 
부족 심화 속 2027년향 HBM 가격 인상 및 예상을 상회하는 수율, 1Q27 진행될 모바
일 DRAM 가격 인상 등을 반영했기 때문이다. LTA 가격 역시 개별 약정된 범위 내 
시장 상황을 반영할 수 있는 구조라는 점을 감안하면, 지속 상승할 것이라 전망한다.

높은 주주환원 의지 확인, 높아진 실적 가시성, 지나친 저평가 
투자의견 매수, 목표주가 4,000,000원을 유지한다. SK하이닉스는 LTA에 대해 기
존 5년에서 추가 연장에 대한 논의가 진행 중이며, 2029년까지 HBM, DRAM은 사실
상 완판, HBM 수요는 공급 가능 물량의 2 배를 상회, 2030년까지 메모리 수요 우위 
국면 전망을 밝혔다. AI 인프라 투자의 강력한 명분과 메모리 수요의 구조성, 잠재적으
로 매우 높은 LTA 위반 비용을 감안하면 실적 가시성과 안정성 제고가 동반되는 국면
이다. 100조원 수준의 주주환원은 2026년 예상 FCF의 66% 수준, 분기별 자사주 
매입, 소각 지속 계획을 통해 높은 주주환원 의지를 보여주고 있다. 실적 Duration 장
기화, 주주환원을 감안하면 12MF P/E 3.4X 수준의 현 주가는 현저한 저평가 국면이
다. 과도한 우려의 되돌림과 메모리 밸류에 대한 재논의 국면 진입을 전망한다.

2026-10-29

작성자(한동희)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

제약업종

◼ 3Q26 컨센서스 부합, 4Q26 성장폭 확대 전망 
◼ 5공장 Ramp-up 지속, 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용)

3Q26 Preview: 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 전망 
3Q26 매출액 1조 3,380억원(+6.4% yoy, +1.3% qoq), 영업이익 5,890억원(-
7.0% yoy, +0.4% qoq, OPM 44.0%)으로 컨센서스에 부합하는 실적을 전
망함. 3분기 평균 원/달러 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락하고, 지난 5월 
파업에 따른 생산 차질 영향이 반영되나, 5공장 PPQ 물량의 매출 인식이 
시작되며 yoy, qoq 외형 성장은 이어질 것으로 예상함.  
파업 당시 생산이 지연된 물량은 3Q 중 추가 생산되어 4Q26부터 매출에 반
영될 전망임. 5공장은 다수 프로젝트의 PPQ가 진행 중이며, Ramp-up에 
따라 매출 기여도 점진적으로 확대될 것으로 예상함. 이에 4Q26에는 파
업 이연 물량 반영과 5공장 매출 확대에 따라 실적 성장폭이 확대될 전
망임. 2026년 연간 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 5조 5,310억원(+21.4% 
yoy), 2조 4,780억원(+19.8% yoy, OPM 44.8%)으로 전망함.

신규 수주 회복 기대, 중장기 성장 기반은 확대 중 
관세 관련 불확실성으로 둔화됐던 고객사와의 수주 논의가 재개되고 있음. 
10월 2일 유럽 소재 제약사와 935억원 규모의 CMO 계약을 추가 체결
했으며, 4Q26 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목함.  
PolyPeptide 인수는 연내 거래 종결을 목표로 진행 중이며, 인수 완료 
시 펩타이드 분야로 CDMO 포트폴리오가 확대될 전망임. 6 공장도 연내 
착공을 목표로 준비 중임. 
한편, 노사 리스크는 단기 불확실성 요인임. 노조는 10/26부터 3주간 2
차 총파업을 예고함. 1차 대비 예정된 파업 기간이 길어진 만큼 실제 파
업 진행 시 생산 차질 규모가 주요 변수임. 다만 파업 개시 전까지 협상 
가능성이 열려 있어, 향후 노사 협상 진행 경과를 확인할 필요가 있음.

(단위: 십억원, %)

Document 268: AI股重挫而等权标普逆势涨1.2%，必需消费领涨并一度创新高

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI股重挫而等权标普逆势涨1.2%，必需消费领涨并一度创新高.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-31
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

瑞银的 Mark Paski ，剔除 AI 股的标普 500 成分股本⽉有望上涨 6% ，为 2022 ⾄ 2023 年 AI 交
易开始升温以来的最佳⽉度表现。随着利率⾛低、企业盈利保持韧性，上涨正从少数消费
⻰头扩散到更多股票。
摘要：
周五早盘， AI 股再遭重挫，纳斯达克 100 指数下跌 3% ，等权重标普 500 指数却逆势上涨约
1.2% 。指数之下的分化表明，跌幅⾼度集中在⼤型科技股和 AI 相关股票上。
标普 500 指数当时下跌 1.5% 。英特尔录得有记录以来最⼤⽇内跌幅， AI 主导的抛售让本周的科技
股跌势进⼀步加深；⾼盛追踪美国上市 AI 概念股的 GS Broad AI US Index 下跌 4.7% 。
防御板块则受到追捧。必需消费是标普 500 表现最好的板块，上涨 1.64% ，板块指数在纽约午盘
前⼀度创下纪录新⾼。
财报撑起必需消费
标普 1500 必需消费指数盘中最多上涨 2.3% ，临近美东时间 11 点回吐部分涨幅。领涨的是两家刚
交出亮眼财报的公司。

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⾼露洁 - 棕榄盘前⼀度上涨 5.5% ，开盘后涨约 2.5% 。这家家庭及个⼈护理⽤品公司的利润和总销
售额均超过彭博⼀致预期。尽管美国及北美市场表现偏软，但已在改善，强劲的利润和业绩指
引占了上⻛。
零⻝⽣产商亿滋国际盘前涨幅略超 7% ，午盘前上涨约 3% 。公司第三季度业绩超出预期，主要靠
稳健的销量增⻓。
剔除 AI 股，标普 500 本⽉有望涨 6%
瑞银的 Mark Paski 表⽰，剔除 AI 股的标普 500 成分股本⽉有望上涨 6% ，为 2022 ⾄ 2023 年 AI 交易
开始升温以来的最佳⽉度表现。
他认为，随着利率⾛低、企业盈利保持韧性，上涨正从少数⻰头扩散到更多股票。第三季度财
报季初期， 78% 的公司每股收益超出市场预期，但利润率仍是消费类公司的关键问题。
可选消费公司对需求的判断也偏乐观。希尔顿预计，旗下酒店体系的增⻓势头将延续⾄今年余
下时间并进⼊ 2027 年；皇家加勒⽐称， 2027 年的预订进度领先于历史⽔平，价格也更⾼；捷蓝
航空则表⽰， 9 ⽉的强劲表现延续到了 10 ⽉。 Paski 提醒，更⼤的不确定性仍在利润和利润率。

“ 如果市场⼴度继续改善，除了近期的领涨股，基本⾯更强的消费类股票也会越来越具备配置价
值。 ” 他写道。
从周五的盘⾯看，投资者是在把资⾦从 AI 交易挪向防御类股票，并没有恐慌性地全⾯撤离股市。
即便 AI 相关交易遭到重创，普通个股依然站得住。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

### Page 4

◆ The economy remains on the recovery track despite weakness in some
areas
Annualized real GDP growth in the second quarter was +1.1% QoQ, with growth
staying in positive territory for three straight quarters. Although personal
consumption exhibited weakness and capital investment declined, overall growth
was lifted by foreign demand. Exports, particularly of services, increased, and the
drop in imports of Middle Eastern crude oil also helped foreign demand turn positive.

◆ Housing starts are weak
Housing starts surged 18.6% YoY in June. They have continued to rise on a YoY
basis, but the jump was mainly due to a rebound from the slump in the same month
of the previous year. The recovery in the number of starts is actually moderate,
reflecting higher interest rates and soaring construction costs.
◆ Earthquake impact will need to be watched
Damage from the earthquake that occurred in Kumamoto Prefecture at the end of

July is weighing on economic activity in the affected areas. Although moves toward
recovery can be seen, with many companies having already resumed operations,
the impact could become prolonged in the following scenarios:
The first is if damage to capital stock such as factories and roads has been severe.
Some companies have sustained damage to production facilities, making an early
resumption of operations difficult in some cases.
The second is if a downturn in tourism demand occurs. According to some media

reports, there has been an uptick in cancellations of accommodation reservations in
Kumamoto Prefecture and neighboring prefectures since the earthquake. After the
2016 Kumamoto earthquake, the decline in travel demand from Japanese visitors
was short lived, but lower demand from foreign tourists persisted for a long period.
Annualized real GDP growth was +1.1% quarter over quarter (QoQ) in Q2 2026

### Page 5

Monthly Report of Prospects for Japan's Economy August 2026
The Japan Research Institute, Limited
Exports and inbound demand remain solid despite weakness in some areas
◆ Goods exports are on the verge of picking up
Goods exports are treading water. A sharp rise in memory chip prices is among
factors pushing up export prices, resulting in record high levels of exports in value
terms recently. On the other hand, the increase in volume terms is limited. One

possible reason is that Japan's export competitiveness has declined in fields such as
semiconductors, where global demand is growing.
Looking ahead, exports are expected to pick up. Capital investment by chipmakers
is expanding worldwide, and this should lift exports of semiconductor manufacturing
equipment, an area in which Japan has strength. However, continued caution is
needed regarding the effects of prolonged turmoil in the Middle East. A global

slowdown due to elevated crude oil prices could weigh on exports.
◆ Reluctance to visit among Chinese travelers is weighing on demand for
travel to Japan
Inbound demand is currently soft. The Chinese government's advisory urging its
citizens to refrain from traveling to Japan is having an adverse impact. The number
of Chinese visitors to Japan has fallen to about half the level prior to the advisory.
Meanwhile, the number of tourists from countries other than China remains firm.

Inbound demand is expected to stay robust going forward. Continued increases in
tourists from countries other than China will remain a supporting factor. The
expectation that the yen will remain weak will also act to push up inbound
consumption. However, there are two downside risks going forward:
The first is that the Chinese government keeps the advisory in place for a
prolonged period. When China banned group travel to South Korea in 2017, tourist

numbers stayed low for two to three years.
The second is the impact of higher crude oil prices. Major airlines have embarked
on phased increases in fuel surcharges, which could weigh on inbound demand
during the summer.

### Page 6

Personal consumption is gradually recovering thanks to a favorable income environment
◆ Higher disposable incomes are supporting personal consumption
Personal consumption continues to gradually recover. While consumption of
non-durable goods remains weak, services consumption is firm, particularly in
amusement-related services. In addition, consumption of durable goods has
increased significantly. Temporary factors such as the abolition of the environmental

performance tax on automobiles (since April 2026) and the tightening of
energy-efficiency standards for air conditioners (effective April 2027) have also
contributed to this rise.
A favorable income environment can also be pointed to as a background factor
behind the recovery in personal consumption. YoY real disposable income growth has
generally been positive since mid-2024, and the pace of increase has recently
accelerated. Employee remuneration is rising, partly as a result of agreements made

during the shunto (spring wage negotiations), and net asset income is also increasing
at a faster clip as companies hike dividends in line with stock price rises.
◆ Stock price developments could be a source of concern for personal
consumption
Personal consumption is expected to continue its gradual recovery going forward. In
addition to the favorable income environment and government policy measures to
curb price increases, a recovery in consumer confidence driven by easing tensions in

the Middle East will also provide a tailwind. Recently, the Consumer Confidence Index
has been showing signs of bottoming out.
However, a reverse wealth effect stemming from falling stock prices is a potential
cause for concern. The Nikkei Stock Average, which reached a new high in June, has
been struggling to push higher since the start of July. Estimates suggest the wealth
effect is concentrated among elderly households, but in recent years working-age

households have also increased their holdings of equity-related assets, and the
positive wealth effect for them may have supported consumption to some extent. For
this reason, if stock prices were to fall significantly, there is a danger of downward
pressure on consumption across a wide range of households.

### Page 7

セン
To
◆ The unemployment rate has settled in the mid-2% range
While the employment situation is generally favorable, weakness can be seen in
some areas. The unemployment rate has remained in the mid-2% range since 2022.
The number of employed persons in non-manufacturing, where business conditions
are favorable, is holding firm, while in manufacturing the number remains at a level
below that seen before the COVID-19 pandemic.
In the background to the drop in manufacturing employment is a slump in

production activity. Manufacturing output has been shrinking due to the effects of
U.S. tariff policy, soaring raw material prices, and a decline in the international
competitiveness of Japanese firms. As a result, pressure to reduce headcounts has
been building. Even in non-manufacturing, some industries closely linked to
manufacturing, such as transportation and wholesale/retail, are also exhibiting
weakness in labor demand.
◆ The employment situation could remain fragile in the near term

Looking ahead at the employment situation in the short term, a sense of
stagnation may persist. The contraction in output in the manufacturing sector will
continue to weigh on labor demand. Although production activity has been brisker
recently, particularly in semiconductor-related industries, the employment-creation
capacity of such industries is small. The employment-inducement coefficient per unit
of new demand tends to be lower than in other sectors, meaning that the industries

lack the strength to serve as drivers of employment.
However, in the medium to long term, the unemployment rate is expected to see a
gradual decline. Demand for labor is projected to stay solid, especially in
face-to-face services such as medical/nursing care and accommodation and
eating/drinking services, but the number of people seeking employment from the
non-labor force population has declined significantly due to the expansion in elderly
and female workforce participation. With limited room for expansion in the labor

supply, the job market is expected to tighten further.

The government is curbing inflation
◆ Policy factors are pushing down the inflation rate
In June, core inflation (all items, less fresh food) was 1.6%, staying under 2%
for the fifth consecutive month. Food price inflation has slowed, and a major

reason for this is a fallback in rice prices, which soared last year. Gasoline
subsidies are also continuing to curtail energy price increases.
Inflation looks set to hover in the 2% range in the second half of 2026. Although
energy subsidies and other government measures will soften inflationary pressure
to some extent, with imported inflationary pressure building, prices are expected
to be pushed up as higher costs are passed through to selling prices. From

FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items will
serve to keep prices down, and inflation is expected to drop into the 1% range.
◆ The Bank of Japan (BOJ) will keep its policy rate unchanged
At its July monetary policy meeting, the BOJ decided to keep its policy rate
unchanged.
Long-term interest rates remain high. This reflects concerns about inflation
resulting from the increase in oil prices and expectations of an expansion in fiscal

expenditure.
The BOJ is expected to step up the pace of interest rate hikes while closely
monitoring the impact of higher rates on the real economy and financial/capital
markets. The next rate hike is expected to be in September.
Long-term interest rates look likely to rise gradually, reflecting heightened
anticipation of rate hikes by the BOJ and increased fiscal spending by the
government. If jitters about a deterioration in fiscal discipline resulting from such

measures as energy subsidies or the consumption tax cut take hold, there is also
a risk that long-term interest rates will rise.

Figu

◆ Surging crude oil prices have become a source of upward pressure on
prices
With crude oil prices having spiked against the backdrop of the worsening situation
in the Middle East, corporate input costs have risen sharply, and pressure for price
pass-through is intensifying. Looking at the Final Demand-Intermediate Demand
price indexes, the margin of price increases at the upstream stage far exceeds that at
the downstream stage, and price pass-through pressure is also substantial at the

downstream stage.
According to estimates based on past data, the impact of rising oil prices involves
a time lag. Specifically, the push-up effect on the consumer price inflation rate
increases for around 10 months and continues to exert influence for a long period
thereafter.
◆ Price pass-through willingness is also stronger than before
Notably, food product manufacturers are planning to raise prices for a huge
number of items during the summer and beyond. According to Teikoku Databank, the

number of items slated for price increases as of the end of July, when it conducted its
latest survey, was higher than in the May and June surveys.
If government subsidies for electricity/gas bills and gasoline prices are scaled back
or ended, both energy and food prices will increase concurrently, and the consumer
price inflation rate may rise again.
Attention also needs to be paid to the possibility that price pass-through driven by
elevated oil prices will become prolonged. In the 2020s, the rate of price increases in

transactions between companies has been higher than in the 2010s, and firms'
medium-term outlooks for selling prices have also shifted upward. Events that trigger
cost increases, such as heightened geopolitical tensions, have been occurring
repeatedly, and companies may be more willing to pass through higher costs to
prices than they were before.

### Page 10

The economy is expected to continue to recover, but downside risks are present
◆ The economy is projected to stay on the recovery track
The Japanese economy should continue its gradual recovery. WTI crude oil
futures prices are expected to fall moderately going forward.
In the corporate sector, government support for investment is providing a tailwind,
and demand for investment for labor saving and digital transformation remains
robust. In addition, rising global demand for AI-related goods should support exports.

Supply chain disruptions resulting from the deterioration in the Middle East situation
will begin to ease, and corporate production activity is expected to gradually recover.
In the household sector, inflationary pressure is expected to strengthen from the
summer onward due to higher crude oil prices. In response, the government will
probably ease the inflationary pressure through measures such as energy subsidies.
While the pace of increase in personal consumption is likely to slow somewhat,

consumption is expected to remain resilient amid continued high wage growth. From
FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items should also
support personal consumption.
◆ The Middle East situation and rising interest rates constitute risks
Real GDP growth is projected to remain solid, registering +0.8% in FY2026 and
+0.9% in FY2027.
However, if the situation in the Middle East worsens again or becomes prolonged,
there is a risk that downward pressure on the economy will intensify, mainly through

three channels: 1) weaker exports, 2) raw material supply constraints, and 3) rising
prices. In addition, if market concerns about the fiscal stance of the Takaichi
government grow, the yen could weaken further, and long-term interest rates could
rise, potentially forcing the scaling-back or scrapping of expansionary policies such
as investment support measures, energy subsidies, and lower consumption tax.
Monthly Report of Prospects for Japan's Economy August 2026

The Japan Research Institute, Limited- ８ -

王翊安 
wade.wang@ctbcsis.com

2026 TPCA ： AI 帶動 PCB 全 鏈 規 格 升 級 
AI 規 格 升 級 延 續 ， 材 料 供 給 偏 緊 支 撐 漲 價 
⚫ 高速材料 朝 M9/M10 推進，石英布 需求明確  
⚫ 玻纖布及銅 箔供給偏緊， CCL 漲價延 續至 2027 年 
⚫ NPO 為光通訊 過渡主流， PCB 層數與製 程同步升級  
台光電 (2383) ：公司為 M8 世代首家量產之銅箔基板 (CCL) 供應商，主
要應用於 1.6T 交換器及 AI 伺服器， 2026 年 M9(EM -896) 亦為首家量
產 ， 其中EM-8962 鎖定 ASIC 應用 ， 預計1Q27 量產 。 規格方面 ，EM-892
系列以上屬 M8 等級， K 代表搭配低介電 (Low Dk) 玻纖布，交換器採
用 K2，UBB 因板面積較大改採 K3；K3 及 K4 皆為石英布方案， K4
的 Dk/Df 較前代改善約 50% ，898K4 目前送樣認證中，預計 1Q27 提
供樣品。 M10 送樣中，預計 2026 年完成認證、 2027 年進入 NPI，M10
以 pure 為主 ，M8/M9 則以 hybrid 為主，以兼顧成本與剛性。 PTFE 雖
電性佳，但缺乏玻布支撐，良率與電鍍仍待改善，公 司 不 發 展 PTFE ，
聚焦於既有 HDI 架構下之材料開發。  
載板材料方面， EM-8963 具低熱膨脹係數 (CTE) 特性，已達載板水 準 ，
先導入光模組應用。目前載板材料市場以日韓廠為主，但日韓廠有技
術、缺產能，公司憑藉 HDI 基礎，具良率控制優勢，預期切入速度快。
CPO 方面，公司認為短期 NPO 仍為主流，客戶不會採用缺乏信用紀
錄的材料，公司兼具客戶信任與量產良率； 3.2T scale out 亦有機會採
用 M10。產能方面，公司目前月產能約 600 萬張，預計 2–3 年內翻倍，
2027 年新增之 315 萬張產能全數為 M8 以上高階產品，有助於提高高
階材料占比。  
圖表一、台光電產品線

資料來源：台光電  
德宏 (5475) ： 公司主要產品為石英纖維紗 (Q 紗)及石英纖維布 (Q 布)，
台灣廠目前僅生產 Q 紗，蘇州廠則同時具備 Q 紗及 Q 布產線。台灣 Q
布擴產方面，織布機將於 4Q26 起陸續裝機，預計 3Q27 台灣開始生產
Q 布，初期產能與大陸廠相當，後續依市場狀況逐步增加織布機台數。
織布機自下單到交機需 2–3 年，設備交期為擴產的主要限制。原料方
面，石英純度高 ，原料不會缺貨，一根石英棒可拉出 2–8 萬米纖維。
技 術 方 面 ， 公司 石 英 纖維 專 利 為 自 有， 技 術 路線 與 台 玻(1802) 不同，
拉紗製程需長期累積經驗。另外，因 E 布價格上漲，公司已在中國廠
恢復 E 布生產，因設備仍保留，可較快復產並受惠於 E 布漲價。

### Page 2

【中信投顧投資分析報告】 
產評報告

2026/ 10 /27

2 
 客戶認證方面，公司 Q 布切入時間較早， CCL 廠皆已送樣，部分已完成驗證、部分仍在驗證中， Q 紗亦需經終端客戶驗證，目前全球皆有 Q 紗客戶。公司表示產
品皆已可生產，關鍵在客戶是否採購，目前客戶尚未正式下單。獲利結構方面，
Q 布的單價及毛利率皆優於 Q 紗，台灣廠 2027 年開始自產 Q 布後，有助於改善
產品組合及獲利結構。需求方面，以台光電 (2383) 板材為例，通常需使用 4 層以
上 Q 布；公司亦認為 Q 布為低軌衛星的最佳材料方案，散熱性能較佳，應用不限
於 AI 伺服器。  
圖表二 、德宏石英纖維紗

資料來源： 德宏

臻鼎 -KY(4958) ： 公司此次展出 AI 伺服器、高階 HDI 、高速光通訊、低軌衛星及
Physical AI 等 PCB 產品。 AI 主板方面，展出 24 層 N+M 及 34 層厚板 HDI ，採用
M8/M8U 低損耗材料，板厚達 3–5mm 以上，最高電鍍深寬比達 42:1 ；GPU 板方
面，展出 34 層 6 階 HDI，孔徑深寬比 25:1，並採混合材料設計，中間層為電源層，
兼顧訊號傳輸與供電需求，隨層數、階數與板厚同步提升，製程難度及產品價值
亦同步提高。其他應用方面，公司展出 14 層低軌衛星 PCB 、12 層 mSAP 摺疊手
機板，以及 AI PC 、ADAS 、機器人與 AI 眼鏡等產品，應用領域由消費性電子延
伸至車用與 Physical AI 。 
光通訊方面，公司展出 800G、1.6T、XPO 及 NPO 模組板，800G 採 10–12 層，1.6T
及 XPO 採 12–16 層， NPO 達 14–18 層，並導入 mSAP 、埋孔與 Cavity 製程，最
細線寬線距達 25/30μm ，傳輸速率每升一世代，層數及製程精細度皆同步提升。
技術路線方面， CPO 目前主要由輝達 (NVIDIA) 推動，但終端客戶不希望供應鏈集
中於單一供應商， NPO 因此成為折衷方案，並帶動 SLP 類載板需求增加；公司認
為目前真正具量產趨勢的是 NPO ，各技術之間並非互相取代，最終取決於應用場
景。架構方面， 2026 年 CPO 主要應用於 scale out ，scale up 目前仍以可插拔方案
為主，考量功耗與材質，公司預期未來 scale up 用量將達 scale out 的 10 倍以上。

資料 來源： 臻鼎

聯茂 (6213) ： 公司重點放在 M9/M10 有布之碳氫 (HC)/PPE 方案， M10 採碳氫樹
脂搭配二代 Q 布，目前送樣中，約需 1.5 年才會放量； M11 亦 以 碳 氫 樹脂 為 主 ，
公司 認為碳氫系列為長期主流。 M9 採碳氫樹脂搭配 Q 布及 HVLP4 銅箔，主要
用於 GPU 大廠加速器 ， 預計 1H27 放量 。 產品結構方面 ，2026 年以 M4/M6 為主，
2027 年主力轉向 M7/M8/M9 ，PCIe Gen6 CPU 所需之 M7 預計 2027 年初放量。
光通訊方面， Low CTE 產品線已送驗 CPO 應用，目前板廠認證中；低軌衛星方
面，1H27 將供應 Meiko 地面站用板， M4 加 M7 合計單月約 30 萬張。產能方面，
集團月產能預計由 2026 年底的 570 萬張提升至 2027 年底 630 萬張、 2028 年底
825 萬張、 2029 年底 915 萬張，擴產以無錫廠及泰國廠為主。

目前公司稼動率約 75%，主要受玻纖布短缺限制，缺料程度依序為 E-glass、Low 
Dk2 、Low Dk1 ，原預期 2H27 舒緩，現看來可能一路偏緊至 2028 年，待陸廠玻
纖布產能開出後，稼動率改善將加速財務表現。漲價方面， 3Q26 漲幅超過 15% ，
之後每季以此幅度為基準持續調漲至 2Q27 ，漲價效應將延續至 2027 年中至下
半年； HVLP4/5 隨需求放大亦將出現短缺，銅箔廠 10 月已開始調價，公司將持
續轉嫁成本。我們認為公司高階產品於 2027 年陸續放量，加上漲價效應延續及
產能持續擴充，營運動能可望延續，投資建議買進，目標價 945 元(2027 年 EPS 
× 18 倍 PER) 。

圖表四 、聯茂高速材料

資料來源：聯茂

華通 (2313) ：公司過去營收主要來自手機相關產品，目前正將產線配置由消費性
產品轉向衛星與資料中心產品 ，非消費性產品目前約占總營收 1/3，2027 年有機
會過半。衛星方面，目前約占營收 2 成，主要客戶正進行火箭升級，衛星亦同
步換代至 V3，2027 年衛星板營收有機會較 2026 年倍增，天上板占衛星營收比
重將提升至 4 成以上，視火箭升級進度，甚至有機會過半。擴產方 面，未來三
年以台灣及泰國為主，台灣大園新廠生產 mSAP 光模組板，觀音廠生產 HDI 及
高層數板 (HLC) ，供 AIDC 加速器及交換器使用；泰國第二廠已完工， 2027 年可
投入量產，第三廠為軟板廠，對應消費性與機器人應用。

光模組方面，插拔式產品採 6 次壓合 HDI ，800G 採 9 次壓合 Any -layer ，2028
年可做到 22 層搭配 mSAP 之 3.2T 產品。網通與資料中心營收於 2Q26 及 3Q26
皆已占集團總營收 10% ，預期 4Q26 光模組明顯增量， 2027 年隨新產能逐季開
出，光模組營收規模將快速成長，網通與資料中心營收規模有機會超越衛星產
品。技術路線方面， CPO 屬載板範疇，公司未投入，並認為 CPO 仍有成本等問
題，過渡期間方案競爭仍多。 AI 加速卡方面，隨運算能力提升，板子層數及階
數同步增加，公司目前可做到 44 層 9 階 HDI ，後續將推進至 56 層 10 階。原料
方面， CCL 供應仍偏緊，漲價可能持續，但隨替代料源陸續獲終端客戶採用，
供應彈性已逐步增加。整體來說，我們正面看待公司後續營運狀況。

林裕禮 /IP、半導體

聯發科 2454 TT ( 電子上游–IC 設計 ) 
件 ) 
金麟化龍，再登巔峰 
投資與評價 
1. ASIC 貢獻優於預期、以及 smart edge platform 挹注下，抵銷手機下
滑影響，預估 2026 年營收將年增 14.2%。 
2. 預估 ASIC 貢獻將於 2027 更加驚豔，上修營收貢獻至 5000 億台幣水
準，手機則呈現回溫態勢，預估 2027 年營收年增 83.3%。 
3. 預估聯發科 2026/2027 年 EPS 為 73.85/171.17 元，考量 ASIC 成長
性，AI 能見度高且延續性佳，以歷史本益比區間中間偏上位置 28 倍為
評價基礎，目標價 4800 元，維持買進投資評等。 
重點更新 
AI 成長優於預期，抵銷手機疲弱影響，上修 2026 年營收至年增 14.2% 
手機業務在 3Q26 年季減 1%，聯發科轉向高端機種來因應原物料成本上升
問題，在客戶陸續推出搭載聯發科 SoC 的新 AI 旗艦手機下，研究部修正預
估手機整年衰退約 16.2%。smart edge platforms 今年主要受惠於 AI 平板 、
AI PC 及車用座艙等專案量產，預估整年營收年增 13.5%，power IC 業務受
惠資料中心相關業務市占提升，研究部上修整年成長約為 5%。今年 AI ASIC
研究部預估貢獻從 20.8 億美元上修至 25.1 億美元營收，佔整體營收 11.4%
客戶需求優於預期，且獲得供應鏈的支援。整體而言，預估 2026 年營收隨
著在 AI ASIC 以及 smart edge platforms 貢獻下，可抵銷手機下滑影響，並
上修成長幅度，營收預估年增 14.2%，達 6804.5 億元，毛利率因成本上升以
及 ASIC 毛利較低，略為下滑至 46.2%。 
AI ASIC 迎來爆發期，算力需求令人驚艷，上修 2027 營收年增 83.3% 
AI ASIC 強進需求將延續至 2027 年整年，而根據調研下一代 ASIC 產品不管
在規模與定價皆高於第一個專案，推估將於 1Q28 進入量產 ，2027 年來自資
料中心 ASIC 營收上修至貢獻超過 5000 億台幣規模(約多 1200 億元)，營收
佔比將來到四成水準，佔比高於先前約 6%。而隨著 AI 導入生活以及相關應
用需求的增長下，算力及高效連結雲端為必備功能，手機部分 在低基期下預
估將成長約 23%。而在邊緣 AI 部分，隨著代理 AI 趨勢，邊緣設備迎來結構
性增長，預估維持高速成長態勢。研究部預估 2027 年營收隨著在 AI ASIC 爆
發及手機回溫下，營收將年增83.3%，突破一兆大關，並且毛利率雖因 ASIC
占比較高下略有稀釋，但 ASIC 營業利益率較高，整體提升至 23.17%。

資料來源：台灣指數公司，國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 2

訪談內容 
需求毫無疑慮，成長順勢而起 
手機方面，AI 落地需求以及作為日常生活必需品，研究部認為代理 AI 創新加速發展，公司正與
Android 生態系合作，推動整合至 SoC，並看好其應用延伸至 wearable、mobile、IoT、PC 與
automotive。

在 smart edge platforms 方面，搭載 N1X 的 NVIDIA RTX Spark AI PC 於 10 月中旬透過主要
PC OEM 夥伴上市，結合 NVIDIA GPU 與聯發科 SoC，支援裝置端代理式 AI。Dimensity 9500
搭載於 Samsung Galaxy T ab S12系列 AI 平板，為 Samsung AI 平板連續第三代採用聯發科旗
艦 SoC。

AI ASIC 方面，第二款 AI 加速器封裝方案執行進展良好，目標較前代提高數倍每瓦效能並降低總
持有成本，部分晶粒已完成 tape-out，較大且複雜的晶粒仍依時程推進，將與美國 IDM 封裝夥
伴及台、日基板供應商合作改善良率，並爭取需求上行所需產能，研究部預估 1Q28 進入量產。

ASIC 亦已取得不同於既有主要客戶的第二家資料中心 ASIC 客戶，專案已啟動，預計2028 年開
始量產及貢獻營收， 另外資料中心業務將從晶片延伸至系統與機櫃 ， 透過NVIDIA NVLink Fusion
將客製化 XPU 接入 NVLink 生態系，並支援 UALink，降低部署複雜度、縮短上市與營收實現時
間，並持續推進高速互連、先進封裝與 CPO，448Gbps SerDes 已完成 tape-out，後續進行量
測、驗證及互通性開發，服務更廣泛客戶。

表一、聯發科 2027 年修正預估與前次預估比較

資料來源：國泰證期研究部預估

### Page 3

聯發科 ESG 政策 
聯發科主要從事 IC 設計 ， 產業類別歸類在半導體行業中 ， 依據專門為投資人設計的永續揭露框架 SASB
準則，半導體行業關注重點為下列幾項：溫室氣體排放、能源管理、水和廢水管理、廢棄物和危險材料
管理、員工健康與安全、員工多元化和包容性、產品設計與生命週期管理、材料採購與效率。由於聯發
科為無廠 IC 設計公司，研究部將篇幅著重在產品設計面，並因員工對於IC 設計公司而言是相當重要的
資產，故員工相關議題也會著重於下描述，其餘溫室氣體排放及水和廢水管理等則於後簡述。

1. 員工健康與安全： 
為確保承攬商施工安全管理 ， 對於入場施工之人員均辦理職業安全衛生講習 ， 進行危害告知及宣導安全
施工規範，以預防職業災害發生。2022 年參與安全講習之承攬商共有 793 人並達成年度零災害，零
意外、零損失之目標。

圖 1 員工失能、職業病、缺席比率

資料來源:聯發科、國泰證期研究部整理

2. 員工多元化和包容性： 
聯發科技重視研發創新及國際化營運，視全球人才布局為技術研發的驅動力。截至  2022 年年底，聯
發科技員工總人數為 18,993 人。為有效運籌全球研發能量，聯發科技在全球設有 32 個辦公據點，
海外人員數占總人數約 35.4%。期待透過全球人才交流合作，加速產品開發時程與技術支援即時性。

圖 2 員工年齡架構 
資料來源:聯發科、國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 4

3. 溫室氣體排放： 
聯發科技能資源使用所排放之溫室氣體，排放來源包含外購電力、公用設備、鍋爐、冷卻水塔、冰水主
機等，主要排放源為範疇二的外購電力占整體 93.27%。在公司營運規模持續成長的表現下，致力提高
能源使用效率與規劃再生能源使用，以降低公司營運所產生的溫室氣體排放量。2022 年度主要減量作
為包含持續提升資料中心之能效，及完成既有辦公室據點照明更新為 LED 等，以提高用電效率。

圖 3 2022 年溫室氣體排放情形

資料來源:聯發科、國泰證期研究部整理

4. 水和廢水管理： 
聯發科技之辦公室取水來自各區自來水廠供水，因今年度員工人數增加，且新建置 5 個辦公據點以及 
IT 資料中心之擴充 ， 共取用 485,629 公噸 （約 486 百萬公升） 水量 ， 其取水量相較於前一年增加 27%，
也因此無法達成 2021 年所設定之目標 5%，雖總用水量提升，聯發科技持續進行水資源項目改善計
畫，平均節水比例達 3.7%，未來將持續搭配進行各項用水減量措施，達成每年減少用水 5% 的目標。
廢水主要為生活污水 ， 依各區納管統一由廢水處理廠處理後排放至自然水體 ， 不會對當地環境產生影響
與衝擊，2022 年排放 202,792 公噸（約 203 百萬公升）廢水，其廢水量相較於前一年增加12%，與
前述用水量增加之原因相同。

圖 4 用水量、節水量與節水比例圖  
資料來源:聯發科、國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 28 日

1/3 
タイ中銀、インフレ加速も景気支援を重視して４会合連続で金利据え置き 
～原油高やバーツ安など物価上昇リスクがくすぶるなか、難しい政策運営が続くであろう～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ タイ銀行（中央銀行）は、10 月 28 日に開催した定例の金融政策委員会において、政策金利
を４会合連続で1.00％に据え置くことを決定した。 同国では、 イラン情勢の悪化による原油
高などを背景にインフレが加速しているものの、内需の弱さを反映して需要面からの物価上
昇圧力は限定的とみられる。 
○ 一方で、世界的なＡＩ関連需要の拡大が一部産業を押し上げているものの、家計債務の大き
さや雇用環境の脆弱さなどが個人消費の重しとなり、景気回復の恩恵は一部にとどまってい
るとの見方を示している。さらに、原油高による対外収支の悪化や洪水対策に伴う財政負担
の増大、通貨バーツ安なども景気や物価に対する不透明感を強めている。 
○ こうしたなか、中銀は足元の金利水準が景気回復を支えるうえで適切との認識を示し、イン
フレの上昇は一時的との見方を維持するなど、景気支援を優先する姿勢を示した。ただし、
エルニーニョ現象やイラン情勢を巡る不透明感に加え、金融緩和の長期化に伴うリスクも意
識されるなか、先行きの政策運営は引き続き難しい局面が続くと見込まれる。

タイ銀行（中央銀行）は、10 月 28 日に開催した定例の金融政策委員会において、政策金利を４
会合連続で1.00％に据え置くことを決定した。同行は、 イラン情勢が緊迫化する直前に開催した２
月会合において、同国経済のコロナ禍からの回復が ASEAN（東南アジア諸国連合）主要国のなかで
も遅れていることに加え、 物価の低迷が続いていることなどを背景に利下げに踏み切った。しかし、
直後にイラン情勢が緊迫化したことで状況は一変した。その結果、 中銀は金利を据え置く様子見姿
勢を維持している。

### Page 2

2/3 
タイでは、2025 年半ば以降のインフレ率はマイナスで推移していたものの、2026 年４月以降は一
転してプラスに転じている。 直近９月の
インフレ率は前年同月比＋ 2.82％と中
銀の目標レンジ （２±１％） 内にとどま
るものの、 上限に近付くなどインフレ圧
力が強まっている様子がうかがえる （図
１） 。コアインフレ率は前年同月比＋
1.50％にとどまるものの、2023 年５月以
来の高い伸びとなるなど、 インフレの動
きが広がりをみせている可能性がある。
とはいえ、 コアインフレ率は依然として
低水準にあり、 需要面からの物価上昇圧
力も限定的とみられることが、中銀の様子見姿勢を後押ししたと考えられる。 
金融市場においては、原油高が対外収支を悪化させるとともに、政府による家計支援を目的とし
た補助金政策などに伴う財政悪化への懸念も重なり、経済のファンダメンタルズ（基礎的条件）の
脆弱化が警戒されてきた。さらに、タイでは９月中旬以降、豪雨による洪水被害が広がり、政府は
洪水対策を目的 に財政 支援を拡充し た
ため、 公的債務残高のＧＤＰ比は法定上
限（70％） に近付いている （注１） 。 こう
した事情も重なり、 通貨バーツ相場は調
整の動きを強めて いる（図２） 。タイで
は、伝統的に金取引が活発であり、バー
ツ相場は 年明け直後にかけて金価格に
連れ高する形で強含んだ。しかし、足元
では金価格が調整の動きを強めており、
こうした動きも バーツ安を後押しして
いる可能性がある。バーツ安は輸出競争
力を高める一方、 輸入物価の上昇を通じてインフレ圧力を増幅させることが懸念される。 とはいえ、
足元では景気の不透明要因が山積しており、中銀にとっては様子見姿勢を維持せざるを得ない事情
もうかがえる 
会合後に公表した声明文では、今回の決定が「全会一致（出席した６名の政策委員が据え置きを
支持し、 １名が欠席）」 であったことを明らかにした。 タイ経済については 「ＡＩ （人工知能） 関連
の好循環が景気の勢いを増している」 ものの、 「経済成長率は潜在成長率を下回り、 景気拡大の恩恵

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告

りません。
3/3
は一部に集中している」との認識を示した。一方で、物価動向については「供給要因を理由に一時
的な上昇が見込まれる」 とした。 信用動向については 「全体的な伸びは加速している」 ものの、 「中
小企業向け融資は引き続き縮小している」ほか、 「中小企業や家計向け融資の質を注視する必要が
ある」との認識を示した。そのうえで、中銀の政策運営について「緩和的な金融政策と的を絞った
金融措置は景気回復を下支えしている」 と評価しつつ、 「金利を据え置き、 イラン情勢やエルニーニ
ョ現象によるインフレリスクの影響を注視する必要がある」とした。
具体的には、 「2026 年の経済成長率は＋2.4％、2027 年は＋2.1％になる」 との見通しを示す一方、

「その恩恵はＡＩに関連する製造業やサービス業の企業・労働者に集中している」とした。個人消
費については「家計債務の大きさ、脆弱な雇用環境、低所得世帯を中心とする購買力の弱さが重し
になっている」との見方を示した。加えて、「原油高が外国人観光客数の足かせになる」としたほ
か、「全体としての経済成長が潜在成長率を下回るなかで、中小企業は依然事業環境への適応や競
争激化という困難に直面している」との認識を示した。
物価動向については「2026 年のインフレ率は＋2.1％、2027 年は＋1.3％になる」との見通しを
示し、 「エネルギー価格やエルニーニョ現象の影響で 2027 年初めにかけて上昇が続く」一方、 「そ
の後は構造的な制約要因による低成長や内需の弱さを反映して鈍化する」 とした。 コアインフレ率
も「2026 年は＋1.2％、2027 年は＋1.3％になる」との見通しを示すとともに、 「中期的なインフレ
期待は依然として目標レンジ内に定着している」 とした。 ただし、 「イラン情勢の動向、 補助金財政

の余力低下、 企業によるコスト転嫁の影響、 中期的なインフレ期待による上振れリスクを注視する」
とした。
バーツ相場については「ＦＲＢ（米連邦準備制度理事会）による引き締め観測や原油高が重しに
なっている」 とした。 一方で、 「国際的な金利上昇の動きにあわせてタイでも長期金利が上昇してい
る」ものの、 「民間部門の借入コストは概ね安定している」ほか、「大企業向けをけん引役に信用動
向は回復しているが、 中小企業向け融資は縮小している」、「中小企業や低所得者層をはじめ家計の
債務返済能力を注視する必要がある」との認識を示した。
先行きの政策運営については「物価安定と持続可能な経済成長、金融安定の維持を目指す」との
従来からの考え方を繰り返した。 そのうえで、 「足元の金利水準が景気回復を支えるうえで適切」と
して、景気支援を優先する考えを示した。物価動向については「供給要因による物価は上昇してい
るが、 今後もインフレ見通しや関連するリスクを注視する」 と８月の前回会合と同じ文言を繰り返

すなど、インフレが一時的なものにとどまるとの認識を維持している。一方、今回は「低金利が長
期的なマクロ金融安定に及ぼす影響」 にも言及するなど、 金融緩和の長期化に伴うリスクにも一定
の警戒姿勢を示した。とはいえ、 先行きはエルニーニョ現象の影響が長期化する懸念が高まってお
り、 世界経済や国際金融市場を巡る不透明要因も山積するなか、 政策の舵取りは引き続き難しい局
面が続くと考えられる。
以 上

フィリピン中銀、原油高とペソ安の「二重苦」に直面し、大幅利上げに舵 
～インフレ圧力の高まりを受けて利上げ幅を拡大、金融引き締めの長期化も～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ フィリピン中銀は、10 月 22 日の定例会合で政策金利を50bp 引き上げ、5.50％とすることを
決定した。４会合連続の利上げとなり、今回は利上げ幅を拡大するなど、金融引き締めの度
合いを強化した。背景には、イラン情勢の悪化に伴う原油高や、金融市場における通貨ペソ
安の進行を受けてインフレ圧力が強まったことがある。９月のインフレ率は前年比＋7.2％と
中銀の目標を大幅に上回っている。中銀はインフレ率が 2027 年も高水準で推移し、2028 年
に目標中央値の３％に近付くとの見通しを示した。レモロナ総裁は、今回の利上げが為替安
定を目的とするものではないと強調する一方、追加利上げや緊急利上げの可能性を排除しな
い姿勢を示した。先行きもイラン情勢の不透明感やペソ安圧力が続く可能性があり、さらな
る金融引き締めを迫られる展開も予想される。

フィリピン中央銀行は、10 月 22 日に開催した定例の金融政策委員会において、政策金利である
翌日物リバースレポ金利を 50bp 引き上げ、5.50％とすることを決定した。同行は、４月の定例会
合で約２年半ぶりの利上げに舵を切り、その後も６月、 ８月の定例会合で追加利上げを実施してお
り、今回で４会合連続の利上げとなる。さらに、今回は利上げ幅を拡大させるなど金融引き締めの
度合いを強めており、政策金利も2025 年６月以来の高い水準となった。 
背景には、イラン情勢の悪化による原
油高が世界的なエネルギーインフレを招
くなか、同国は原油需要の９割以上を輸
入に依存しており、その影響に晒されや
すいことがある。直近９月のインフレ率
は前年比＋7.2％と中銀の目標レンジ （２
～４％）を大きく上回っている（図１） 。

支を悪化させており、金融市場では通貨
